# 中英证券及金融术语词汇

**索引：** [[learning-notes/personal-english-book/README|个人英文材料书索引]]
**相关：** [[learning-notes/personal-english-book/work/金融和商务💰_20260514_2037|金融和商务词表]] · [[learning-notes/personal-english-book/work/专业术语|工作专业术语]] · [[learning-notes/tv-series/the-pursuit-of-happyness/vocabulary|当幸福来敲门 · 本片词汇]]（*stockbroker / internship / fund* 情景对照）

> 面向中国大陆读者整理。正文采用中国大陆监管与行业常用术语；香港专有制度、机构、产品、规则和系统集中列入附录。原始材料来自 2011 年香港版 PDF 抽取稿，已清理页码、断行与 OCR 噪音。

## 使用说明

- 每条词条只归入一个主分类；同一中文词在不同语境下对应不同英文时分别保留。
- “其他说法/缩写”保留港式旧称、常见异名和缩写；法域专有词在备注中标明。
- IPA 采用美式发音，只为多音节、重音不直观、含不发音字母或容易误读的常用复杂术语补注；简单词、纯缩写及大多数机构专名以“—”表示。
- 本词库保留 2011 年来源中的历史机构和系统名称；备注为“香港市场专有术语”的词条不代表中国大陆现行制度。

## 市场与交易 { #market-trading }

| No. | 中国大陆术语 | 英文 | IPA（美） | 其他说法/缩写 | 备注 | 示例句 |
|---:|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 按市价计值 | mark to market / mark-to-market | — | 按市值计价 | — | Our risk team will **mark to market** every derivative position at the close of each trading day. — 我们的风控团队会在每个交易日收市时把所有衍生品持仓**按市价计值**。 |
| 2 | 暗池交易 | dark pool | — | 黑池交易 | — | The broker routed the large order to a **dark pool** to avoid moving the price. — 经纪商把这笔大额订单发到**暗池交易**平台，以免冲击价格。 |
| 3 | 暗盘价 | grey market price | — | — | — | Investors watched the **grey market price** closely to gauge demand before the shares officially debuted. — 投资者密切关注**暗盘价**，以便在股份正式上市前判断市场需求。 |
| 4 | 扒头交易 | front running; trading ahead | — | — | — | The dealer was dismissed for **front running**, buying shares just before executing a huge client order. — 该交易员因**扒头交易**被解雇，他在执行客户巨额订单前抢先买入股票。 |
| 5 | 本地伦敦金 | Loco-London gold | — | — | — | The bank quoted **Loco-London gold** for settlement through the London bullion clearing system. — 该银行报出**本地伦敦金**价格，通过伦敦金银结算系统交收。 |
| 6 | 边远值 | outliers | — | — | — | Before running the regression, the analyst removed several **outliers** that distorted the average returns. — 分析师在做回归分析前剔除了几个扭曲平均回报的**边远值**。 |
| 7 | 标准合约 | standard contract | — | — | — | Futures exchanges trade a **standard contract** with fixed size, quality and delivery terms. — 期货交易所买卖的是规模、品质及交收条款都固定的**标准合约**。 |
| 8 | 标准组合交易功能 | standard combination trading function | — | — | — | Traders use the **standard combination trading function** to enter both legs of a spread in one step. — 交易员使用**标准组合交易功能**，一次过输入价差交易的两边。 |
| 9 | 标准组合买卖盘挂盘纪录 | Standard Combination Order Book | — | — | — | Combination orders waiting to be matched are displayed in the **Standard Combination Order Book**. — 等待配对的组合买卖盘会显示在**标准组合买卖盘挂盘纪录**中。 |
| 10 | 并行买卖 | parallel trading | — | — | — | During the counter change, the old and new shares were available for **parallel trading** for a period. — 更换柜台期间，新旧股份在一段时间内可以**并行买卖**。 |
| 11 | 驳脚经纪 | runner | — | — | — | In the old trading hall, a **runner** carried order slips between the phone desks and the floor. — 在旧交易大堂，**驳脚经纪**负责在电话台和交易场之间传递买卖单。 |
| 12 | 补购 | buy in | — | — | — | When the seller failed to deliver, the clearing house had to **buy in** the shares on the market. — 卖方未能交收时，结算所只好在市场上**补购**相关股份。 |
| 13 | 不动盘 | inactive order | — | — | — | An **inactive order** sitting in the queue can be reactivated by the broker before the session ends. — 排队中的**不动盘**可由经纪在时段结束前重新启动。 |
| 14 | 不能配对买卖盘 | order imbalance | — | — | — | A large **order imbalance** at the opening auction pushed the stock well above its previous close. — 开市竞价时出现大量**不能配对买卖盘**，把股价推高至远超前收市价。 |
| 15 | 不停电电源装置 | uninterrupted power supply (UPS) | — | 不间断电源装置 | — | The data centre relies on an **uninterrupted power supply (UPS)** to keep trading servers running during outages. — 数据中心依靠**不停电电源装置**，在停电时维持交易服务器运作。 |
| 16 | 部分完成的买卖指示 | partially filled order | — | — | — | Only half of my **partially filled order** was executed before the price moved away. — 价格变动前，我这张**部分完成的买卖指示**只成交了一半。 |
| 17 | 财经市场发展专责小组 | The Financial Market Development Task Force | — | — | — | **The Financial Market Development Task Force** advises the government on strengthening Hong Kong's financial markets. — **财经市场发展专责小组**就加强香港金融市场的发展向政府提供意见。 |
| 18 | 财务通融 | financial accommodation | — | — | — | The company sought **financial accommodation** from its bankers to bridge a temporary cash shortfall. — 公司向往来银行寻求**财务通融**，以应付短期资金缺口。 |
| 19 | 财务租赁 | financial lease | — | — | — | The airline acquired new aircraft through a **financial lease** instead of paying cash upfront. — 该航空公司通过**财务租赁**取得新飞机，而非一次性现金支付。 |
| 20 | 参考草拟本 | indicative draft | — | — | — | Lawyers circulated an **indicative draft** of the term sheet so both sides could comment early. — 律师发出条款清单的**参考草拟本**，让双方及早提出意见。 |
| 21 | 仓 | position | — | — | — | She decided to close her **position** in the stock after it hit her target price. — 股价达到目标价后，她决定平掉这只股票的**仓**。 |
| 22 | 差额缴款 | marks / marks payment | — | — | — | The clearing house collects **marks** from members whose futures positions lost value overnight. — 结算所向期货持仓隔夜亏损的会员收取**差额缴款**。 |
| 23 | 差价 | spread | — | — | — | Market makers profit from the **spread** between the prices at which they buy and sell. — 庄家从买入价和卖出价之间的**差价**中获利。 |
| 24 | 场内交易 | pit trading | — | — | — | Before electronic systems, **pit trading** involved brokers shouting and using hand signals on the floor. — 在电子系统出现前，**场内交易**靠经纪在交易场内大声喊价和打手势进行。 |
| 25 | 场内交易产品 | exchange traded product | — | — | — | An ETF is a popular **exchange traded product** that tracks an index at low cost. — 交易所买卖基金是一种以低成本追踪指数的热门**场内交易产品**。 |
| 26 | 场内买卖盘 | on-floor order | — | — | — | The trader keyed the **on-floor order** into the terminal located in the exchange hall. — 交易员在交易大堂内的终端机输入这张**场内买卖盘**。 |
| 27 | 场外交易 | off-floor trading; off-board trading | — | — | — | Some block deals are negotiated through **off-floor trading** and then reported to the exchange. — 部分大额交易通过**场外交易**议定，然后再向交易所申报。 |
| 28 | 场外交易议价板 | OTC Bulletin Board | — | — | 适用/来源：US。 | Many small US companies had their shares quoted on the **OTC Bulletin Board** rather than a major exchange. — 许多美国小型公司的股票在**场外交易议价板**而非主要交易所报价。 |
| 29 | 场外证券市场 | over-the-counter market | — | — | — | Most corporate bonds change hands in the **over-the-counter market** between dealers. — 大部分公司债券是在交易商之间的**场外证券市场**买卖的。 |
| 30 | 超连结 | hyperlink | — | — | — | Click the **hyperlink** in the announcement to download the full annual report. — 点击公告中的**超连结**即可下载完整年报。 |
| 31 | 超买 | overbought | — | — | — | After a ten-day rally, technical indicators suggested the stock was **overbought**. — 连续十日上涨后，技术指标显示该股已**超买**。 |
| 32 | 超卖 | oversold | — | — | — | Bargain hunters stepped in once the index looked **oversold** after the sharp sell-off. — 急跌之后指数看来**超卖**，趁低吸纳的投资者开始入市。 |
| 33 | 成交单 | contract note | — | 成交单据；买卖单据 | — | The broker emails a **contract note** to the client after each trade is executed. — 每笔交易执行后，经纪都会把**成交单**电邮给客户。 |
| 34 | 成交调整 | trade amendment | — | — | — | The back office submitted a **trade amendment** to correct the wrong account number. — 后勤部门提交**成交调整**，更正错误的户口号码。 |
| 35 | 成交额比率 | turnover ratio | — | — | — | A high **turnover ratio** indicates that the fund manager trades the portfolio frequently. — **成交额比率**偏高，表示基金经理频繁买卖投资组合。 |
| 36 | 成交及取消 | Fill and Kill | — | — | 适用/来源：限价盘。 语境：限价单。 | A **Fill and Kill** order executes whatever it can at the limit price and cancels the rest immediately. — **成交及取消**指令会在限价内尽量成交，余下部分即时取消。 |
| 37 | 成交纪录 | trading record | — | — | — | Regulators may request the firm's complete **trading record** during an inspection. — 监管机构在检查时可要求公司提交完整的**成交纪录**。 |
| 38 | 成交确认委托 | transaction confirmation order | — | — | — | Both parties must enter a **transaction confirmation order** before the system confirms the bilateral deal. — 双方必须输入**成交确认委托**，系统才会确认这笔双边交易。 |
| 39 | 程序买卖 | algorithmic trading; programme trading; black box trading | /ˌælɡəˈrɪðmɪk ˈtreɪdɪŋ/ | 交易 | — | Hedge funds increasingly use **algorithmic trading** to execute orders in milliseconds. — 对冲基金越来越多利用**程序买卖**在毫秒间执行指令。 |
| 40 | 迟报交易 | late reported sales | — | — | — | Compliance flagged several **late reported sales** that were entered well after execution. — 合规部标记了几宗在执行后很久才输入的**迟报交易**。 |
| 41 | 持仓 | position | — | — | — | 同「仓」（No. 21） |
| 42 | 持仓比例 | position ratio | — | — | — | The exchange publishes the **position ratio** of large holders to improve market transparency. — 交易所公布大户的**持仓比例**，以提高市场透明度。 |
| 43 | 持仓净额 | net position | — | — | — | After offsetting longs and shorts, the desk's **net position** in gold was small. — 抵销多空仓后，交易台的黄金**持仓净额**不大。 |
| 44 | 持仓上限 | position limit | — | 限额 | — | No single investor may exceed the **position limit** set for each futures contract. — 任何投资者都不得超过每种期货合约所设的**持仓上限**。 |
| 45 | 持仓限额比率 | position limit ratio | — | — | — | The broker monitors each client's **position limit ratio** to warn them before they breach the cap. — 经纪监察每位客户的**持仓限额比率**，在其超出上限前发出警告。 |
| 46 | 持续关联交易 | continuing connected transaction | — | 持续关连交易 | — | The supply agreement with its parent is a **continuing connected transaction** requiring annual review. — 与母公司签订的供应协议属于**持续关联交易**，须每年审阅。 |
| 47 | 持续交易时段 | continuous trading session | — | — | — | Most trades are matched automatically during the **continuous trading session**. — 大部分交易在**持续交易时段**内自动配对。 |
| 48 | 持续专业培训课程 | Continuous Professional Training (CPT) | — | — | — | Licensed representatives must complete **Continuous Professional Training (CPT)** hours every year. — 持牌代表每年必须完成规定时数的**持续专业培训课程**。 |
| 49 | 出市代表 | floor trader; authorised clerk | — | 出市员 | — | The **floor trader** relayed the client's instructions to the trading terminal on the exchange floor. — **出市代表**把客户指示传送到交易大堂的交易终端。 |
| 50 | 除权交易 | ex-entitlement trading | — | — | — | From the first day of **ex-entitlement trading**, buyers are no longer entitled to the dividend. — 从**除权交易**首日起，买家不再享有该次股息。 |
| 51 | 处理时延 | order processing latency | — | — | — | The firm upgraded its servers to cut **order processing latency** for high-frequency clients. — 公司升级服务器，为高频交易客户缩短**处理时延**。 |
| 52 | 穿价交易 | trade through | — | — | — | Rules on **trade through** require orders to be executed at the best available price. — 有关**穿价交易**的规则要求指令须按最佳可得价格执行。 |
| 53 | 纯交易板 | trading-only board | — | — | — | A **trading-only board** allows securities to be traded without a full primary listing. — **纯交易板**容许证券在没有完整第一上市的情况下买卖。 |
| 54 | 次要控制人 | minority controller | — | — | — | As a **minority controller**, he must still disclose his significant stake to regulators. — 作为**次要控制人**，他仍须向监管机构披露其重大持股。 |
| 55 | 存留利息 | retention interest | — | — | — | Securitisation rules require the originator to keep a **retention interest** in the issued notes. — 证券化规则要求发起人在发行的票据中保留**存留利息**。 |
| 56 | 错价交易 | error trade | — | — | — | The exchange reviewed the **error trade** executed far away from the prevailing market price. — 交易所审查了这宗成交价远离当时市价的**错价交易**。 |
| 57 | 错误包容 | fault tolerance | — | — | — | The trading platform is built with **fault tolerance** so a single server failure won't halt the market. — 交易平台设有**错误包容**能力，单一服务器故障不会令市场停顿。 |
| 58 | 错误交易 | erroneous trade | — | — | — | The exchange can cancel an **erroneous trade** if both parties agree it was a mistake. — 若双方都同意出错，交易所可以取消该宗**错误交易**。 |
| 59 | 错误交易合约 | mistraded contract | — | — | — | The member reported a **mistraded contract** and asked the exchange to adjust the price. — 该会员申报了一张**错误交易合约**，要求交易所调整价格。 |
| 60 | 打和点 | breakeven point | — | — | — | The stock must rise another five percent to reach my **breakeven point** after fees. — 扣除费用后，这只股票还要再涨百分之五才到我的**打和点**。 |
| 61 | 大额买卖盘 | block order | — | — | — | The pension fund split its **block order** into smaller pieces to limit market impact. — 退休基金把**大额买卖盘**拆细，以减低对市场的冲击。 |
| 62 | 大宗交易 | block trade | — | 大手交易 | — | Two institutions agreed a **block trade** of one million shares off the order book. — 两家机构在挂盘册以外议定一宗一百万股的**大宗交易**。 |
| 63 | 大宗交易机制 | Block Trade Facility; Block Trading Facility (BTF) | — | 大宗交易设施；大手交易机制 | — | Large derivatives deals can be registered through the exchange's **Block Trade Facility**. — 大额衍生品交易可以通过交易所的**大宗交易机制**登记。 |
| 64 | 代客买卖 | agency trade | — | — | — | In an **agency trade**, the broker acts on the client's behalf and earns a commission. — 在**代客买卖**中，经纪代表客户行事并赚取佣金。 |
| 65 | 代客买卖账户 | agency account | — | 代客买卖户口 | — | Client orders are booked in an **agency account** separate from the firm's own trades. — 客户指令记入与公司自营交易分开的**代客买卖账户**。 |
| 66 | 代理人 | agent | — | — | — | The bank acts as **agent** for the issuer in distributing coupon payments to bondholders. — 该银行担任发行人的**代理人**，向债券持有人派发票息。 |
| 67 | 贷出费 | lending fee | — | — | — | Short sellers pay a **lending fee** to the institution that lends them the shares. — 卖空者须向借出股份的机构支付**贷出费**。 |
| 68 | 单币单股 | Single Tranche, Single Counter (STSC) | — | — | — | Under **Single Tranche, Single Counter (STSC)**, each listed security is traded in one currency on one counter. — 在**单币单股**安排下，每只上市证券只以一种货币在一个柜台买卖。 |
| 69 | 单一价格竞价市场 | single price auction system | — | — | — | The opening auction works as a **single price auction system**, matching all orders at one equilibrium price. — 开市竞价采用**单一价格竞价市场**机制，以单一均衡价配对所有买卖盘。 |
| 70 | 淡仓 | short position | — | — | — | The hedge fund built a large **short position** expecting the shares to fall. — 对冲基金预期股价下跌，建立了大量**淡仓**。 |
| 71 | 当日有效盘 | day order | — | 即日买卖盘；即日盘 | 义项语境：市场与交易，对应 day order。 | A **day order** that is not filled by the close is automatically cancelled. — 收市前仍未成交的**当日有效盘**会被自动取消。 |
| 72 | 当日有效盘 | Rest of Day | — | — | 适用/来源：限价盘。 义项语境：市场与交易，对应 Rest of Day。 语境：限价单。 | She placed a **Rest of Day** limit order, so any unfilled part stays in the queue until the close. — 她输入**当日有效盘**限价单，未成交部分会留在轮候队伍直至收市。 |
| 73 | 到期 | maturity; expiry date | — | 到期日 | — | Investors receive the bond's face value at **maturity** along with the final coupon. — 投资者在债券**到期**时收回面值及最后一期票息。 |
| 74 | 到期前有效买卖盘 | good-till-date order (GTD) | — | — | — | A **good-till-date order** stays active until the date the client specifies. — **到期前有效买卖盘**会一直生效，直至客户指定的日期。 |
| 75 | 低收费经纪 | discount broker | — | — | — | Cost-conscious investors often choose a **discount broker** that offers execution without research. — 注重成本的投资者常选择只提供执行服务、不提供研究的**低收费经纪**。 |
| 76 | 低于面值 | below par | — | — | — | When rates rose, the bond traded **below par** at around ninety-five. — 利率上升后，该债券以约九十五的价格**低于面值**买卖。 |
| 77 | 低于最佳卖盘价 | downtick; minus tick | — | 低于最好沽盘价 | — | Some markets ban short selling on a **downtick** to prevent pressure on falling prices. — 部分市场禁止在**低于最佳卖盘价**时卖空，以免加剧跌势。 |
| 78 | 抵销 | set off | — | — | — | The clearing house will **set off** amounts owed against amounts due before settling the balance. — 结算所会把应付款与应收款**抵销**，然后才结算差额。 |
| 79 | 点子 | basis point | — | — | — | The central bank cut its policy rate by twenty-five **basis points**. — 中央银行把政策利率下调了二十五个**点子**。 |
| 80 | 电子交易 | electronic trading | — | — | — | **Electronic trading** has largely replaced the shouting crowds on the exchange floor. — **电子交易**已大致取代了交易大堂内的喧闹人群。 |
| 81 | 电子直达市场安排 | direct market access | — | 非参与者直接落盘 | — | With **direct market access**, the client's orders go straight to the exchange through the broker's system. — 通过**电子直达市场安排**，客户指令经经纪系统直接送到交易所。 |
| 82 | 垫款 | advance; monies advanced | — | — | — | The broker charged interest on the **advance** made to the client to settle the purchase. — 经纪就为客户交收而提供的**垫款**收取利息。 |
| 83 | 调息日 | coupon-reset date | — | — | — | On each **coupon-reset date**, the floating-rate note's interest is adjusted to the benchmark. — 在每个**调息日**，浮息票据的利息会按基准利率调整。 |
| 84 | 调整 | adjustment | — | — | — | The option contract terms underwent an **adjustment** after the company's bonus issue. — 公司派送红股后，期权合约条款作出了**调整**。 |
| 85 | 调整持仓 | position adjustment | — | — | — | The clearing member requested a **position adjustment** to move contracts to the correct client account. — 结算会员申请**调整持仓**，把合约转到正确的客户户口。 |
| 86 | 调整基数 | adjusted basis | — | — | — | Your capital gain is calculated from the **adjusted basis** after accounting for splits and fees. — 资本收益是按计及拆股和费用后的**调整基数**计算的。 |
| 87 | 跌停板措施 | circuit breakers | — | — | — | Trading was halted for fifteen minutes when the **circuit breakers** were triggered. — **跌停板措施**被触发后，交易暂停了十五分钟。 |
| 88 | 定价模式 | pricing model | — | — | — | The desk uses a **pricing model** to value illiquid options that lack market quotes. — 交易台利用**定价模式**为缺乏市场报价的低流通量期权估值。 |
| 89 | 冻结 | locked up | — | — | — | The pre-IPO shares are **locked up** for six months after listing. — 上市前股份在上市后六个月内被**冻结**。 |
| 90 | 独资经营者 | sole proprietor | — | — | — | As a **sole proprietor**, he is personally liable for all the firm's debts. — 作为**独资经营者**，他须个人承担公司的所有债务。 |
| 91 | 短期同业拆放市场 | call market | — | — | — | Banks short of funds overnight can borrow in the **call market** from banks with surplus cash. — 隔夜资金短缺的银行可在**短期同业拆放市场**向资金充裕的银行借款。 |
| 92 | 对盘 | order matching | — | — | — | The exchange's engine performs **order matching** based on price and time priority. — 交易所系统按价格及时间优先次序进行**对盘**。 |
| 93 | 对盘前时段 | pre-order matching period | — | — | — | No new orders may be entered or cancelled during the **pre-order matching period**. — 在**对盘前时段**内不得输入或取消买卖盘。 |
| 94 | 对盘时段 | order matching period | — | — | — | Orders are matched at the final equilibrium price during the **order matching period**. — 在**对盘时段**内，买卖盘按最终均衡价配对。 |
| 95 | 对敲 | bucketing | — | — | — | The firm was fined for **bucketing**, taking the other side of client orders without executing them on the market. — 该公司因**对敲**被罚，即自己承接客户指令而不在市场执行。 |
| 96 | 对手方 | counterparty | /ˈkaʊntɚˌpɑːrti/ | — | — | Before entering a swap, the bank assessed the credit risk of its **counterparty**. — 订立掉期合约前，银行评估了**对手方**的信贷风险。 |
| 97 | 对手经纪 | contra broker | — | — | — | The settlement team contacted the **contra broker** to resolve the mismatched trade details. — 交收团队联络**对手经纪**，处理交易资料不符的问题。 |
| 98 | 囤仓策略 | buy and hold strategy | — | — | — | Long-term investors often follow a **buy and hold strategy** rather than trading frequently. — 长线投资者通常采用**囤仓策略**，而非频繁买卖。 |
| 99 | 多边交易设施 | multilateral trading facility (MTF) | — | — | — | A **multilateral trading facility** brings together multiple buyers and sellers outside a traditional exchange. — **多边交易设施**在传统交易所以外撮合多名买家和卖家。 |
| 100 | 多头 | long position; bull position | — | — | — | He holds a **long position** in oil futures, betting that prices will rise. — 他持有原油期货的**多头**，押注油价会上升。 |
| 101 | 多种货币买卖 | multi-currency trading | — | — | — | The platform supports **multi-currency trading**, so clients can buy stocks in Hong Kong dollars or yuan. — 该平台支持**多种货币买卖**，客户可用港元或人民币买股票。 |
| 102 | 饵盘 | bait order | — | — | — | Regulators warned that placing a **bait order** to lure others into trading is market manipulation. — 监管机构警告，落**饵盘**引诱他人交易属于操纵市场。 |
| 103 | 二级市场 | secondary market; after market / aftermarket | — | — | — | After the IPO, the shares began trading on the **secondary market** among investors. — 首次公开招股后，股份开始在投资者之间的**二级市场**买卖。 |
| 104 | 二手市场 | used market | — | — | — | Prices in the **used market** for trading terminals fell after the exchange switched systems. — 交易所转换系统后，交易终端的**二手市场**价格下跌。 |
| 105 | 二氧化碳当量 | carbon dioxide equivalent (CO2e) | — | — | — | The fund reports its portfolio emissions in tonnes of **carbon dioxide equivalent**. — 该基金以吨**二氧化碳当量**报告投资组合的排放量。 |
| 106 | 反驳交易 | rejected sales | — | — | — | The counterparty disputed the details, so the deal appeared among the **rejected sales**. — 对手方对交易资料有异议，因此这宗交易列入**反驳交易**。 |
| 107 | 反弹 | rally | — | — | — | Bank stocks led a strong **rally** after the central bank's announcement. — 中央银行宣布后，银行股带动市场强劲**反弹**。 |
| 108 | 反馈测试模式 | beta test mode | — | — | — | Brokers can test their new interface with the exchange in **beta test mode** before launch. — 经纪可以在推出前以**反馈测试模式**与交易所测试新接口。 |
| 109 | 访客廊 | Viewing Gallery | — | — | — | Visitors could watch the trading floor from the exchange's **Viewing Gallery**. — 访客可以在交易所的**访客廊**观看交易大堂。 |
| 110 | 非参与者直接落盘 | direct market access | — | 电子直达市场安排 | — | 同「电子直达市场安排」（No. 81） |
| 111 | 非常重大的出售事项 | very substantial disposal | — | — | — | Selling its main business would count as a **very substantial disposal** requiring shareholder approval. — 出售其主要业务将构成**非常重大的出售事项**，须经股东批准。 |
| 112 | 非即日平仓交易 | overnight trade | — | — | — | Holding a futures contract past the close makes it an **overnight trade** subject to margin calls. — 收市后仍持有期货合约便属**非即日平仓交易**，须承受追加保证金的风险。 |
| 113 | 非金钱利益 | soft-dollar benefits | — | — | — | Fund managers must disclose any **soft-dollar benefits**, such as research received from brokers. — 基金经理必须披露任何**非金钱利益**，例如从经纪获得的研究报告。 |
| 114 | 非实物化 | dematerialisation | — | — | — | **Dematerialisation** means shares exist only as electronic records instead of paper certificates. — **非实物化**指股份只以电子纪录形式存在，而非纸本股票。 |
| 115 | 非市场庄家的交易 | non-market-making transaction | — | — | — | The trader's hedge was a **non-market-making transaction** and did not qualify for the fee waiver. — 该交易员的对冲交易属**非市场庄家的交易**，不能获豁免费用。 |
| 116 | 非整手买卖盘 | round-lot-plus order | — | — | — | A **round-lot-plus order** includes whole board lots plus some extra odd-lot shares. — **非整手买卖盘**包括若干完整手数再加上一些碎股。 |
| 117 | 非正常项目 | abnormal item | — | — | — | Auditors asked management to explain the **abnormal item** that boosted quarterly profit. — 核数师要求管理层解释令季度利润上升的**非正常项目**。 |
| 118 | 非中介化 | disintermediation | — | — | — | Online platforms drive **disintermediation** by letting companies raise money directly from investors. — 网上平台让公司直接向投资者集资，推动**非中介化**。 |
| 119 | 非自动对盘的交易 | manual trade | — | — | — | The broker booked the negotiated deal as a **manual trade** after agreeing terms by phone. — 经纪在电话上议定条款后，把这宗交易记为**非自动对盘的交易**。 |
| 120 | 分行证明书 | branch certificate | — | — | — | The bank issued a **branch certificate** confirming the deposit held at its local office. — 银行发出**分行证明书**，确认存款存放于当地分行。 |
| 121 | 「粉红价单」 | Pink Sheets | — | — | 适用/来源：US。 | Thinly traded US stocks were historically quoted in the **Pink Sheets**. — 交投稀疏的美国股票过去在**「粉红价单」**上报价。 |
| 122 | 疯狂抛售 | bear raid | — | — | — | A coordinated **bear raid** drove the share price down as short sellers dumped stock. — 卖空者联手**疯狂抛售**，把股价压低。 |
| 123 | 付运中货品 | goods-in-transit | — | — | — | The auditor confirmed that **goods-in-transit** at year end were included in inventory. — 核数师确认年结时的**付运中货品**已计入存货。 |

| 125 | 附权交易 | cum-trading | — | — | — | During **cum-trading**, buyers of the shares still receive the upcoming dividend. — 在**附权交易**期间，买入股份的人仍可获得即将派发的股息。 |
| 126 | 复牌 | resumption of trading | — | — | — | The stock jumped twenty percent on the **resumption of trading** after the merger news. — 宣布合并消息后，该股**复牌**时急升百分之二十。 |
| 127 | 复式盘 | spread order | — | — | — | The trader entered a **spread order** to buy March futures and sell June futures together. — 交易员输入**复式盘**，同时买入三月期货和卖出六月期货。 |
| 128 | 杠杆市场 | leveraged market | — | — | — | Forex is a **leveraged market**, so small price moves can cause large gains or losses. — 外汇是**杠杆市场**，细微的价格变动都可能带来巨大盈亏。 |
| 129 | 杠杆效应 | leverage | — | — | — | **Leverage** magnified the fund's losses when the market turned against it. — 市况逆转时，**杠杆效应**放大了基金的亏损。 |
| 130 | 高杠杆效应的投资 | high leveraged investment | — | — | — | Warrants are a **high leveraged investment** and may not suit conservative investors. — 窝轮是**高杠杆效应的投资**，未必适合保守型投资者。 |

| 135 | 个人客户 | individual client | — | — | — | Each **individual client** must complete a risk profile questionnaire before trading derivatives. — 每位**个人客户**在买卖衍生品前都必须填写风险评估问卷。 |

| 145 | 公司盘 | firm order; proprietary trading | — | — | — | The trader marked the order as a **firm order** because it was for the company's own account. — 交易员把该指令标记为**公司盘**，因为是为公司自营户口落盘。 |

| 149 | 公众阅览室 | Public Viewing Room | — | — | — | Investors could inspect listed company documents in the exchange's **Public Viewing Room**. — 投资者可以在交易所的**公众阅览室**查阅上市公司文件。 |
| 150 | 供股 | rights issue | — | — | — | The bank launched a **rights issue** offering one new share for every five held. — 该银行进行**供股**，每持有五股可认购一股新股。 |

| 161 | 关联人士交易 | related party transaction | — | 关连人士交易 | — | The audit committee reviews every **related party transaction** to ensure fair pricing. — 审核委员会审阅每宗**关联人士交易**，确保定价公平。 |

| 164 | 锅炉室 | boiler room | — | — | — | Police raided a **boiler room** where salespeople pressured victims into buying worthless shares. — 警方突击搜查一间**锅炉室**，那里的推销员向受害人强推无价值的股票。 |
| 165 | 国际收支平衡 | balance of payments | — | 国际收支差额 | — | A persistent trade deficit can strain a country's **balance of payments**. — 长期贸易逆差可能令一个国家的**国际收支平衡**承受压力。 |
| 166 | 过渡期受托经纪 | bridging broker; bridging dealer | — | — | — | After the firm's licence was suspended, a **bridging broker** handled clients' outstanding trades. — 该公司牌照被暂停后，由**过渡期受托经纪**处理客户未完成的交易。 |
| 167 | 过量交易 | overtrading | — | — | — | The adviser was disciplined for **overtrading** a client's account to generate commissions. — 该顾问因为赚取佣金而在客户户口**过量交易**，受到纪律处分。 |
| 168 | 还盘 | counter order | — | — | — | The buyer rejected the quote and sent a **counter order** at a lower price. — 买家拒绝报价，并以较低价格发出**还盘**。 |
| 169 | 合成工具 | synthetics | — | — | — | Traders can build **synthetics** from options to replicate a long stock position. — 交易员可以用期权组建**合成工具**，复制持有股票的长仓。 |
| 170 | 合成模拟策略 | synthetic replication strategy | — | 综合复制策略 | — | The ETF follows a **synthetic replication strategy**, using swaps instead of holding the index stocks. — 该交易所买卖基金采用**合成模拟策略**，以掉期代替持有指数成分股。 |
| 171 | 合格境外有限合伙人 | Qualified Foreign Limited Partner (QFLP) | — | — | — | The **Qualified Foreign Limited Partner (QFLP)** scheme lets overseas investors fund private equity in mainland China. — **合格境外有限合伙人**计划让海外投资者投资内地私募股权。 |

| 175 | 合约细则 | contract specifications | — | — | — | Check the **contract specifications** for trading hours, tick size and settlement method before trading. — 买卖前请先查看**合约细则**，了解交易时间、价位及结算方式。 |
| 176 | 合资合同 | joint venture contract | — | — | — | The two firms signed a **joint venture contract** setting out each partner's capital and profit share. — 两家公司签订**合资合同**，订明各方出资及利润分配。 |
| 177 | 合作备忘录 | memorandum of understanding (MOU) | — | — | — | The regulators signed a **memorandum of understanding** to share information on cross-border cases. — 两地监管机构签署**合作备忘录**，就跨境案件互通信息。 |
| 178 | 核准借出代理人 | approved lending agent | — | — | — | Only an **approved lending agent** may lend securities on behalf of clients under the scheme. — 在该计划下，只有**核准借出代理人**可代客户借出证券。 |
| 179 | 核准借入人 | approved borrower | — | — | — | As an **approved borrower**, the firm can borrow shares to cover short sales. — 作为**核准借入人**，该公司可借入股份以弥补卖空。 |
| 180 | 后交易指令 | post-trade transaction | — | — | — | Brokers submit a **post-trade transaction** to allocate executed orders to client accounts. — 经纪提交**后交易指令**，把已执行的指令分配到客户户口。 |

## 发行与上市 { #issuance-listing }

| No. | 中国大陆术语 | 英文 | IPA（美） | 其他说法/缩写 | 备注 | 示例句 |
|---:|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 包销 | underwrite | — | — | — | Three investment banks agreed to **underwrite** the entire share offering at a fixed price. — 三家投资银行同意以固定价格**包销**全部发售股份。 |
| 2 | 包销承担净值 | net underwriting commitment | /nɛt ˈəndərˈraɪtɪŋ kəˈmɪtmənt/ | — | — | The bank's **net underwriting commitment** rose sharply after two co-managers withdrew from the deal. — 两家联席经办人退出交易后，该银行的**包销承担净值**大幅上升。 |
| 3 | 包销商 | underwriter | — | — | — | As lead **underwriter**, the bank must buy any shares that investors do not take up. — 作为**包销商**，该银行必须买入投资者未认购的任何股份。 |
| 4 | 包销银团 | underwriting syndicate | /ˈəndərˈraɪtɪŋ ˈsɪndɪkət/ | — | — | The **underwriting syndicate** spread the risk of the large bond issue across a dozen banks. — **包销银团**把这笔大额债券发行的风险分摊给十多家银行。 |
| 5 | 保荐经纪 | sponsoring broker | — | — | — | The **sponsoring broker** coordinated the company's application and liaised with the exchange on its behalf. — **保荐经纪**协调公司的上市申请，并代表其与交易所沟通。 |
| 6 | 保荐人 | sponsor | — | — | — | The exchange expects the **sponsor** to conduct thorough due diligence before submitting a listing application. — 交易所要求**保荐人**在递交上市申请前进行全面尽职调查。 |
| 7 | 《保荐人声明》 | Sponsor’s Declaration | — | — | — | Before the hearing, the bank filed the **Sponsor’s Declaration** confirming it had completed its due diligence. — 聆讯前，该银行提交了《**保荐人声明**》，确认已完成尽职调查。 |

| 104 | 以先旧后新方式配售 | placing and top-up; top up placing | — | — | — | In a **top up placing**, the major shareholder sells existing shares and then subscribes for new ones. — 在**以先旧后新方式配售**中，大股东先出售旧股，再认购新股。 |
| 105 | 优先认购权 | pre-emptive right | — | 优先购买权 | — | A **pre-emptive right** protects existing shareholders from having their stakes diluted by new issues. — **优先认购权**保护现有股东的持股比例不因新股发行而被摊薄。 |

| 108 | 增补 | supplementary / supplemental listing document | — | 补充上市文件 | — | A **supplementary listing document** was issued to correct an error in the original filing. — 公司刊发**增补**上市文件，更正原文件中的错误。 |
| 109 | 增发 | further issue | — | — | — | The board approved a **further issue** of shares to fund the new factory. — 董事会批准**增发**股份，为新工厂提供资金。 |

| 116 | 证券发售事宜工作小组 | Securities Offering Issues sub-group | — | — | — | The **Securities Offering Issues sub-group** examined ways to streamline the offering process. — **证券发售事宜工作小组**研究简化发售流程的方法。 |
| 117 | 重复认购 | multiple applications | — | — | — | **Multiple applications** for the same offering will be rejected under the prospectus terms. — 根据招股书条款，同一发行的**重复认购**申请将被拒绝。 |
| 118 | 主板 | main board | — | — | — | 同「第一板」（No. 22） |
| 119 | 主承销商 | lead underwriter | /lɛd ˈəndərˌraɪtər/ | — | — | 同「牵头包销商」（No. 71） |
| 120 | 主要保荐人 | primary sponsor | — | — | — | The **primary sponsor** takes the lead in preparing the listing application and answering exchange queries. — **主要保荐人**牵头准备上市申请，并回应交易所查询。 |
| 121 | 主要上市 | primary listing | — | — | — | 同「第一上市」（No. 23） |
| 122 | 资本化发行 | capitalisation issue | — | — | — | Before the IPO, a **capitalisation issue** converted part of the reserves into new shares for existing holders. — 首次公开发行前，公司通过**资本化发行**把部分储备转为新股发给现有股东。 |
| 123 | 最初发起人 | ultimate originator | — | — | — | The disclosure rules require the trust to name the **ultimate originator** of the securitised assets. — 披露规则要求信托列明证券化资产的**最初发起人**。 |

## 清算交收与托管 { #clearing-settlement-custody }

| No. | 中国大陆术语 | 英文 | IPA（美） | 其他说法/缩写 | 备注 | 示例句 |
|---:|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | T + 2 交收制度 | T + 2 settlement system | — | — | — | Under the **T+2 settlement system**, shares bought on Monday are delivered and paid for on Wednesday. — 在**T + 2 交收制度**下，周一买入的股票会在周三完成交付与付款。 |
| 2 | T + 2 日完成款项交收 | T+2 Finality | — | — | — | With **T+2 Finality**, participants know money settlement becomes irrevocable two days after the trade. — 有了**T + 2 日完成款项交收**安排，参与者知道款项交收会在交易后两天成为不可撤销。 |
| 3 | 保管人 | custodian | — | — | — | The pension fund appointed a global **custodian** to hold its overseas securities safely. — 该退休基金委任了一家全球**保管人**来妥善持有其海外证券。 |
| 4 | 被视为截止过户日期 | deemed book close date | — | — | — | Investors holding shares in CCASS by the **deemed book close date** will receive the dividend. — 在**被视为截止过户日期**前通过中央结算系统持股的投资者将获派股息。 |
| 5 | 标准登记服务 | standard registration service | — | — | — | Most brokers use the **standard registration service**, which takes longer but costs less. — 大多数经纪商使用**标准登记服务**，虽然耗时较长但费用较低。 |
| 6 | 补购交易 | buy-in transaction | — | — | — | After the seller failed to deliver, the clearing house executed a **buy-in transaction** in the market. — 卖方未能交付后，结算所在市场上执行了一笔**补购交易**。 |
| 7 | 承让人 | assignee; transferee | — | — | — | 同「获分配人士」（「市场与交易」No. 197） |
| 8 | 持续净额交收 | continuous net settlement (CNS) | — | — | — | **Continuous net settlement** rolls unsettled positions forward and nets them against the next day's trades. — **持续净额交收**会把未交收的持仓滚存，并与次日交易进行净额计算。 |
| 9 | 存管处及共享代理人系统 | Depository and Common Nominee System (DNS) | /dɪˈpɑzəˌtɔri ənd ˈkɑmən ˌnɑməˈni ˈsɪstəm/ | — | — | Shares deposited into CCASS are held through the **Depository and Common Nominee System**. — 存入中央结算系统的股份是通过**存管处及共享代理人系统**持有的。 |
| 10 | 存管代理人 | depository nominee | — | — | — | Legally, the shares are registered in the name of the **depository nominee**, not the end investor. — 从法律上讲，这些股份登记在**存管代理人**名下，而不是最终投资者名下。 |
| 11 | 大量账户转移指示 | Mass Account Transfer Instruction (Mass ATI) | — | 大量户口转移指示 | — | The broker used a **Mass Account Transfer Instruction** to move all client holdings to the new custodian at once. — 经纪商用一份**大量账户转移指示**一次性把所有客户持仓转到新托管行。 |
| 12 | 大批登记服务 | bulk registration service | — | — | — | The custodian submitted thousands of certificates through the **bulk registration service** before the dividend record date. — 托管行在股息登记日前通过**大批登记服务**提交了数千张股票。 |
| 13 | 大批交收程序 | bulk settlement run | — | — | — | Stock positions are settled in the afternoon **bulk settlement run** if participants have enough shares. — 如果参与者有足够股份，股票持仓会在下午的**大批交收程序**中完成交收。 |
| 14 | 档案转换 | file transfer | — | — | — | Operations staff received the daily trade report from the clearing system via **file transfer**. — 运营人员通过**档案转换**从结算系统接收每日交易报告。 |
| 15 | 登记持有人 | registered owner | — | — | — | Only the **registered owner** on the share register is entitled to vote at the general meeting. — 只有股东名册上的**登记持有人**才有权在股东大会上投票。 |
| 16 | 登记过户费 | scrip fee | — | — | — | Clients withdrawing physical certificates from CCASS may have to pay a **scrip fee** to the registrar. — 从中央结算系统提取实物股票的客户可能需要向过户处支付**登记过户费**。 |
| 17 | 第三者结算 | Third Party Clearing (TPC) | — | — | — | Smaller brokers often rely on **Third Party Clearing** instead of clearing their own trades. — 规模较小的经纪商通常依靠**第三者结算**，而不是自行结算交易。 |
| 18 | 电子款项交收费 | electronic money settlement fee | — | — | — | The monthly invoice included an **electronic money settlement fee** for each payment processed through the bank. — 月度账单列明了每笔经银行处理的付款所收取的**电子款项交收费**。 |
| 19 | 电子款项交收服务 | Electronic Money Settlement Services | — | — | — | **Electronic Money Settlement Services** let participants settle cash obligations directly through designated banks. — **电子款项交收服务**让参与者可以直接通过指定银行结清现金责任。 |
| 20 | 电子收付款指示 | Electronic Payment Instruction (EPI) | — | — | — | The clearing house sends an **Electronic Payment Instruction** to the participant's bank to debit the amount due. — 结算所向参与者的银行发出**电子收付款指示**，以扣除应付款项。 |
| 21 | 对外持仓转移 | external position transfer | — | — | — | An **external position transfer** moved the client's futures positions to another clearing participant. — 一项**对外持仓转移**把该客户的期货持仓转到另一家结算参与者。 |
| 22 | 多批交收处理程序 | batch settlement run | — | — | — | Failed deliveries can still be completed in a later **batch settlement run** on the same day. — 未能完成的交付仍可在同日较晚的**多批交收处理程序**中完成。 |
| 23 | 分列登记册 | Split Register | — | — | — | Because the company keeps a **Split Register**, shareholders can hold shares on either the local or overseas register. — 由于公司设有**分列登记册**，股东可以在本地或海外登记册上持股。 |
| 24 | 付款参与者 | paying Participant | — | — | — | The **paying Participant** must have sufficient funds before the settlement instruction can be completed. — **付款参与者**必须有足够资金，交收指示才能完成。 |
| 25 | 隔夜大批结算 | overnight bulk clearing | — | — | — | Trades executed late in the day are processed during **overnight bulk clearing**. — 当天较晚执行的交易会在**隔夜大批结算**中处理。 |
| 26 | 更改财政年度结算日期 | change of year end | — | — | — | The listed company announced a **change of year end** to align its reporting with its parent group. — 该上市公司宣布**更改财政年度结算日期**，以便与母集团的报告期保持一致。 |
| 27 | 股份交收费 | stock settlement fee | — | — | — | A **stock settlement fee** is charged whenever shares move between participant accounts. — 每当股份在参与者账户之间转移时，都会收取**股份交收费**。 |
| 28 | 股份结算费 | stock clearing fee | — | — | — | The broker passes the exchange's **stock clearing fee** on to clients as part of trading costs. — 经纪商把交易所的**股份结算费**作为交易成本的一部分转嫁给客户。 |
| 29 | 股份托管费 | stock custody fee | — | — | — | Long-term investors holding many shares in CCASS should consider the monthly **stock custody fee**. — 在中央结算系统中长期大量持股的投资者应考虑每月的**股份托管费**。 |
| 30 | 国际结算银行 | Bank for International Settlements (BIS) | /bæŋk fər ˌɪnərˈnæʃənɑl ˈsɛtəlmənts/ | — | — | The **Bank for International Settlements** in Basel serves as a bank for central banks. — 位于巴塞尔的**国际结算银行**充当各国中央银行的银行。 |
| 31 | 过户 | give-up | — | — | 适用/来源：期权。 义项语境：清算交收与托管，对应 give-up。 语境：期权。 | The executing broker completed a **give-up** so the options trade would clear through the client's clearing firm. — 执行经纪商完成了**过户**，使该期权交易通过客户的结算公司结算。 |
| 32 | 过户 | Transfer | — | — | 义项语境：清算交收与托管，对应 Transfer。 | The **Transfer** of the shares will not be recorded until the stamp duty has been paid. — 在缴纳印花税之前，这些股份的**过户**不会被登记。 |
| 33 | 过户处 | registrar; share registrar | — | 过户登记处 | — | Shareholders should contact the company's **share registrar** to update their mailing address. — 股东应联系公司的**过户处**更新邮寄地址。 |
| 34 | 过户处参与者账户 | Registrar Participant Account | — | 过户处参与者户口 | — | The registrar uses its **Registrar Participant Account** to handle share movements in CCASS. — 过户处利用其**过户处参与者账户**处理中央结算系统中的股份变动。 |
| 35 | 过户代理 | transfer agent | — | — | — | The fund's **transfer agent** processes subscriptions and redemptions for unit holders. — 该基金的**过户代理**为基金持有人处理认购和赎回。 |
| 36 | 过户费 | transfer fee | — | — | — | Investors pay a **transfer fee** to the registrar for each new share certificate issued. — 每签发一张新股票，投资者都要向过户处支付**过户费**。 |
| 37 | 过户文件 | instrument of transfer | — | — | — | The seller signed the **instrument of transfer** so the buyer could be registered as the new holder. — 卖方签署了**过户文件**，以便买方登记为新持有人。 |
| 38 | 过户纸(转让)印花税 | transfer deed stamp duty | — | — | — | Each off-exchange share transfer is subject to a fixed **transfer deed stamp duty**. — 每宗场外股份转让都须缴纳固定金额的**过户纸(转让)印花税**。 |
| 39 | 货银对付方式交收 | delivery vs payment (DvP) | — | — | — | Under **delivery vs payment**, securities are released only when the cash payment is confirmed. — 在**货银对付方式交收**下，只有确认收到款项后才会交付证券。 |
| 40 | 货银同步交收 | delivery vs payment (DvP) | — | — | — | 同「货银对付方式交收」（No. 39） |
| 41 | 集资净额 | net proceeds | — | — | — | The company will use the **net proceeds** of the share placement to repay bank loans. — 公司将把配股的**集资净额**用于偿还银行贷款。 |
| 42 | 交收 | settlement | — | — | — | The trade was executed on Monday, but **settlement** will not happen until Wednesday. — 该交易于周一执行，但**交收**要到周三才会进行。 |
| 43 | 交收积压 | settlement backlog | — | — | — | Heavy trading volumes created a **settlement backlog** that took operations teams days to clear. — 庞大的成交量造成了**交收积压**，运营团队花了好几天才处理完。 |
| 44 | 交收期 | settlement period | — | — | — | Shortening the **settlement period** reduces counterparty risk across the market. — 缩短**交收期**可降低整个市场的交易对手风险。 |
| 45 | 交收日 | settlement date | — | — | — | Please make sure funds are in your account before the **settlement date**. — 请确保在**交收日**之前资金已存入您的账户。 |
| 46 | 交收上限 | settlement cap | — | — | — | The clearing house imposed a **settlement cap** to limit each participant's daily exposure. — 结算所设定了**交收上限**，以限制每个参与者的每日风险敞口。 |
| 47 | 交收银行 | settlement bank | — | — | — | Each clearing participant must appoint a **settlement bank** to handle its money settlement. — 每个结算参与者都必须指定一家**交收银行**处理其款项交收。 |
| 48 | 交收指示 | settlement instruction (SI) | — | — | — | The custodian input a **settlement instruction** to deliver the shares to the buyer's account. — 托管行输入一项**交收指示**，把股份交付到买方账户。 |
| 49 | 交收指示的交易 | SI transaction | — | — | — | The two brokers settled the block outside the exchange as an **SI transaction**. — 两家经纪商把这笔大宗交易作为**交收指示的交易**在交易所以外交收。 |
| 50 | 「交收指示」交易 | settlement instruction transaction | — | — | — | A **settlement instruction transaction** settles only after both counterparties input matching instructions. — 只有双方输入相符的指示后，**「交收指示」交易**才会交收。 |
| 51 | 交易及交收委员会 | Trading and Settlement Committee | — | — | — | The **Trading and Settlement Committee** reviewed the proposed changes to post-trade procedures. — **交易及交收委员会**审议了拟议的交易后程序变更。 |
| 52 | 交易及结算综合系统 | integrated trading and clearing system | — | — | — | The exchange is upgrading to an **integrated trading and clearing system** to cut processing time. — 交易所正在升级为**交易及结算综合系统**，以缩短处理时间。 |
| 53 | 结算 | clearing | — | — | — | **Clearing** determines each member's obligations before any cash or shares actually change hands. — **结算**是在任何现金或股份实际易手之前，先确定每个会员的责任。 |
| 54 | 结算服务热线中心 | Clearing Service Call Centre | — | — | — | Participants can call the **Clearing Service Call Centre** if they have questions about settlement reports. — 参与者如对交收报告有疑问，可致电**结算服务热线中心**。 |
| 55 | 结算机构参与者 | Clearing Agency Participant | — | — | — | An overseas depository can join CCASS as a **Clearing Agency Participant** to link the two markets. — 海外存管机构可以作为**结算机构参与者**加入中央结算系统，以连接两地市场。 |
| 56 | 结算价 | clearing price | — | — | — | Margin calls are based on the difference between the trade price and the day's **clearing price**. — 追加保证金是根据成交价与当日**结算价**之间的差额计算的。 |
| 57 | 结算所 | clearing house | — | — | — | The **clearing house** steps in between buyers and sellers to guarantee each trade. — **结算所**介入买卖双方之间，为每笔交易提供担保。 |
| 58 | 结算所参与者 | Clearing House Participant | — | — | — | Only a **Clearing House Participant** can settle trades directly with the clearing house. — 只有**结算所参与者**才能直接与结算所交收交易。 |
| 59 | 结算所电子附属登记系统 | Clearing House Electronic Subregister System (CHESS) | — | — | 适用/来源：澳洲。 适用/来源：澳大利亚。 | Australian share ownership is recorded electronically in the **Clearing House Electronic Subregister System**. — 澳大利亚的股份持有记录以电子方式登记在**结算所电子附属登记系统**中。 |
| 60 | 结算协议书 | clearing agreement | — | — | — | The non-clearing broker signed a **clearing agreement** with a bank to settle its trades. — 这家非结算经纪商与一家银行签署了**结算协议书**以交收其交易。 |
| 61 | 结算咨询小组 | Clearing Consultative Panel | — | — | — | The **Clearing Consultative Panel** gives market participants a forum to comment on clearing rules. — **结算咨询小组**为市场参与者提供一个就结算规则发表意见的平台。 |
| 62 | 截止过户期间 | book closure period | — | — | — | No share transfers will be registered during the **book closure period** before the AGM. — 在股东周年大会前的**截止过户期间**，不会登记任何股份过户。 |
| 63 | 截止过户日期 | book close date | — | — | — | Investors must lodge transfer documents before the **book close date** to qualify for the dividend. — 投资者必须在**截止过户日期**前递交过户文件，才能享有股息。 |
| 64 | 净额结算 | netting | — | — | — | **Netting** reduced hundreds of trades to a single payment between the two banks. — **净额结算**把数百笔交易减少为两家银行之间的一笔付款。 |
| 65 | 跨日净额交收 | cross-day netting | — | — | — | **Cross-day netting** lets unsettled positions from earlier days offset new obligations. — **跨日净额交收**使前几日未交收的持仓可以抵销新的责任。 |
| 66 | 款项交收费 | money settlement fee | — | — | — | The bank charges a **money settlement fee** for each payment made on behalf of the participant. — 银行代参与者支付每笔款项时都会收取**款项交收费**。 |
| 67 | 款项净额结算每日平均效率 | daily average funds netting efficiency | — | — | — | The clearing house reports its **daily average funds netting efficiency** to show how much payment volume netting saved. — 结算所公布其**款项净额结算每日平均效率**，以显示净额结算节省了多少付款额。 |
| 68 | 联邦结算系统 | Fed wire; Fedwire; FedWire | — | — | — | The U.S. bank sent the dollar payment through **Fedwire** for same-day settlement. — 这家美国银行通过**联邦结算系统**汇出美元款项，以便当日交收。 |
| 69 | 临时结算表 | Provisional Clearing Statement (PCS) | — | — | — | Participants should check the **Provisional Clearing Statement** for any errors before the final run. — 参与者应在最后一轮处理前检查**临时结算表**是否有错误。 |
| 70 | 另选登记服务 | optional registration service | — | — | — | For urgent cases, the registrar offers an **optional registration service** at a higher fee. — 对于紧急情况，过户处以较高收费提供**另选登记服务**。 |
| 71 | 每日结算价 | daily settlement price | — | — | — | Futures positions are marked to market using the **daily settlement price**. — 期货持仓按**每日结算价**逐日盯市。 |

| 213 | 行权期 | exercise schedule | — | — | — | Employees should check the **exercise schedule** to see when their share options can be exercised. — 员工应查看**行权期**安排，了解其股票期权何时可以行使。 |

## 基金与集合投资 { #funds-collective-investment }

| No. | 中国大陆术语 | 英文 | IPA（美） | 其他说法/缩写 | 备注 | 示例句 |
|---:|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 保本信托基金 | guaranteed unit trust | — | — | — | Retail investors liked the **guaranteed unit trust** because it promised to return their capital at maturity. — 散户喜欢这只**保本信托基金**，因为它承诺到期返还本金。 |
| 2 | 保证基金 | Guarantee Fund | — | — | — | Clients who suffer losses from a broker's default may seek compensation from the **Guarantee Fund**. — 因经纪商违约而蒙受损失的客户可向**保证基金**申请赔偿。 |
| 3 | 闭端式基金 | closed-end fund | — | — | — | Shares of a **closed-end fund** often trade at a discount to its net asset value. — **闭端式基金**的份额常以低于资产净值的价格交易。 |
| 4 | 偿债基金 | sinking fund | — | — | — | The issuer contributes to a **sinking fund** each year to retire the bonds gradually. — 发行人每年向**偿债基金**拨款，以逐步赎回债券。 |
| 5 | 储备基金 | reserve fund | — | — | — | The board set aside part of the annual surplus in a **reserve fund** for unexpected claims. — 董事会将部分年度盈余拨入**储备基金**，以应付意外索偿。 |
| 6 | 传统基金会 | The Heritage Foundation | — | — | 适用/来源：US。 | Analysts at **The Heritage Foundation**, a conservative US think tank, often comment on fiscal policy. — 美国保守派智库**传统基金会**的分析师经常评论财政政策。 |
| 7 | 创投基金/创业基金 | venture capital | — | 创投基金∕创业基金 | — | The startup raised its first round of **venture capital** to expand its engineering team. — 这家初创公司完成了首轮**创投基金**融资，用于扩充工程团队。 |
| 8 | 单位信托基金 | unit trust | — | — | — | Each investor in the **unit trust** owns units whose value tracks the underlying portfolio. — **单位信托基金**的每位投资者持有基金单位，其价值随相关投资组合变动。 |
| 9 | 定息组合股 | Fixed Income ETF | — | — | — | She added a **Fixed Income ETF** to her portfolio to gain diversified bond exposure. — 她在组合中加入了一只**定息组合股**，以获得分散化的债券敞口。 |
| 10 | 对冲基金 | hedge fund | — | — | — | The **hedge fund** used leverage and short selling to profit from the market downturn. — 这只**对冲基金**利用杠杆和卖空在市场下跌中获利。 |
| 11 | 对冲基金的基金 | fund of hedge funds | — | — | — | A **fund of hedge funds** spreads its capital across several managers to reduce single-manager risk. — **对冲基金的基金**把资金分散投给多位管理人，以降低单一管理人风险。 |
| 12 | 反向 | inverse / short exchange-traded fund | — | 淡仓交易所买卖基金 | — | Traders bought an **inverse exchange-traded fund** to profit if the index fell. — 交易员买入一只**反向**交易所交易基金，以便在指数下跌时获利。 |
| 13 | 房地产投资信托基金 | real estate investment trust (REIT) | — | — | — | A **real estate investment trust (REIT)** must distribute most of its rental income to unitholders. — **房地产投资信托基金**须将大部分租金收入分派给基金单位持有人。 |

| 16 | 浮动保证基金 | Dynamic Guarantee Fund | — | — | — | The exchange adjusts each member's contribution to the **Dynamic Guarantee Fund** according to its risk exposure. — 交易所根据各会员的风险敞口调整其对**浮动保证基金**的供款。 |
| 17 | 浮动储备基金 | Dynamic Reserve Fund | — | — | — | Clearing participants must top up the **Dynamic Reserve Fund** when their positions grow larger. — 结算参与者的持仓增加时，必须补缴**浮动储备基金**。 |
| 18 | 公积金计划 | Provident Fund Scheme | — | 公积金计画 | — | New staff are automatically enrolled in the company's **Provident Fund Scheme** after probation. — 新员工过了试用期后会自动加入公司的**公积金计划**。 |
| 19 | 共同基金 | mutual fund | — | — | — | Many workers invest in a **mutual fund** through their retirement accounts for professional management. — 许多上班族通过退休账户投资**共同基金**，以获得专业管理。 |
| 20 | 国际货币基金组织 | International Monetary Fund (IMF) | — | — | — | The **International Monetary Fund (IMF)** provides loans to member countries facing balance-of-payments problems. — **国际货币基金组织**向面临国际收支困难的成员国提供贷款。 |
| 21 | 合成 ETF | synthetic ETF | — | 合成交易所买卖基金 | — | A **synthetic ETF** uses swaps rather than holding the actual index stocks, adding counterparty risk. — **合成 ETF** 通过掉期而非持有实际指数成分股来复制表现，因此增加了交易对手风险。 |
| 22 | 合约互惠基金 | contractual mutual fund | — | — | — | Under a **contractual mutual fund** plan, the investor agrees to contribute a fixed amount regularly. — 在**合约互惠基金**计划下，投资者同意定期投入固定金额。 |
| 23 | 互保基金 | Fidelity Fund | — | — | — | Members contribute to the **Fidelity Fund** so that clients can be compensated if a member firm defaults. — 会员向**互保基金**供款，以便在会员公司违约时赔偿客户。 |
| 24 | 互惠基金 | mutual fund | — | — | — | 同「共同基金」（No. 19） |
| 25 | 豁免基金经理 | Exempt Fund Manager | — | — | — | As an **Exempt Fund Manager**, the firm was not required to hold the usual licence for certain activities. — 作为**豁免基金经理**，该公司在某些业务上无须持有一般牌照。 |
| 26 | 基金份额持有人 | fund unit holder | — | — | — | Every **fund unit holder** receives an annual report detailing the fund's performance and fees. — 每位**基金份额持有人**都会收到一份详列基金表现和费用的年报。 |

| 28 | 集体投资基金 | collective investment fund | — | — | — | Small investors pooled their money in a **collective investment fund** to access institutional-grade assets. — 小投资者将资金汇集到**集体投资基金**中，以投资机构级资产。 |
| 29 | 集体投资计划 | collective investment schemes (CIS) | — | 集体投资计画 | — | Regulators require **collective investment schemes (CIS)** offered to the public to be authorised first. — 监管机构要求向公众发售的**集体投资计划**必须先获认可。 |
| 30 | 交易所交易基金 | Exchange Traded Fund (ETF) | — | 交易所买卖基金 | — | Buying an **Exchange Traded Fund (ETF)** lets you trade a whole index like a single stock. — 买入**交易所交易基金**，就能像买卖单只股票一样交易整个指数。 |
| 31 | 交易型开放式指数证券投资基金 | Exchange Traded Fund (ETF) | — | 交易型开放式指数基金 | — | 同「交易所交易基金」（No. 30） |
| 32 | 开放式基金 | open-end fund | — | — | — | An **open-end fund** issues new units whenever investors put in more money. — 每当投资者追加资金，**开放式基金**就会发行新的基金单位。 |
| 33 | 离岸基金 | offshore fund | — | — | — | The family office held an **offshore fund** domiciled in the Cayman Islands for tax efficiency. — 这家家族办公室持有一只注册在开曼群岛的**离岸基金**，以提高税务效率。 |
| 34 | 联邦基金 | Federal funds | — | — | — | Banks lend **Federal funds** to each other overnight to meet reserve requirements. — 银行之间隔夜拆借**联邦基金**，以满足准备金要求。 |
| 35 | 联邦基金利率 | Federal funds rate | — | — | — | Markets rallied after the Fed signalled it would hold the **Federal funds rate** steady. — 美联储暗示将维持**联邦基金利率**不变后，市场上涨。 |
| 36 | 联合交易所赔偿基金 | Unified Exchange Compensation Fund | — | — | — | Investors harmed by a defaulting exchange participant may claim against the **Unified Exchange Compensation Fund**. — 因交易所参与者违约而受损的投资者，可向**联合交易所赔偿基金**提出索偿。 |

| 38 | 年金 | pension fund | — | — | — | The city's **pension fund** increased its allocation to infrastructure to match long-term liabilities. — 该市的**年金**提高了基础设施配置，以匹配长期负债。 |

| 40 | 期货赔偿基金 | Futures Compensation Fund | — | — | — | Clients of a defaulting futures broker may apply to the **Futures Compensation Fund** for relief. — 违约期货经纪商的客户可向**期货赔偿基金**申请赔偿。 |

| 44 | 认可存款基金 | approved deposit fund | — | — | — | Retirees can roll their lump-sum benefits into an **approved deposit fund** to defer tax. — 退休人士可将一次性领取的福利转入**认可存款基金**，以递延缴税。 |

| 47 | 上市开放式基金 | listed open-end fund (LOF) | — | — | — | A **listed open-end fund (LOF)** can be bought on the exchange or subscribed through the fund company. — **上市开放式基金**既可在交易所买卖，也可通过基金公司申购。 |
| 48 | 实物资产 | ETF physical ETF | — | — | — | A **physical ETF** actually holds the stocks in its benchmark index instead of using derivatives. — **实物资产 ETF** 实际持有基准指数的成分股，而非使用衍生工具。 |
| 49 | 私募股权基金 | privately-offered fund / private equity fund | — | 私募基金 | — | The **private equity fund** acquired a controlling stake in the retailer and plans to take it private. — 这只**私募股权基金**收购了该零售商的控股权，并计划将其私有化。 |
| 50 | 外汇基金票据 | Exchange Fund Bill | — | — | — | Banks buy the **Exchange Fund Bill** as a highly liquid, short-term Hong Kong dollar asset. — 银行买入**外汇基金票据**作为高流动性的短期港元资产。 |

| 52 | 外汇基金债券 | Exchange Fund Note (EFN) | — | — | — | The yield on the ten-year **Exchange Fund Note (EFN)** serves as a benchmark for Hong Kong dollar bonds. — 十年期**外汇基金债券**的收益率是港元债券的基准。 |

## 证券与公司行为 { #securities-corporate-actions }

| No. | 中国大陆术语 | 英文 | IPA（美） | 其他说法/缩写 | 备注 | 示例句 |
|---:|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 按揭证券 | mortgage-backed securities | /ˈmɔːrɡɪdʒ bækt səˈkjʊrətiz/ | — | — | The fund cut its exposure to **mortgage-backed securities** as homeowners began refinancing at lower rates. — 随着房主开始以更低利率再融资，该基金削减了对**按揭证券**的持仓。 |
| 2 | 保本产品 | capital preservation product | — | — | — | For clients who cannot tolerate losses, the adviser suggested a **capital preservation product** with modest upside. — 对于无法承受亏损的客户，顾问推荐了一款收益有限的**保本产品**。 |
| 3 | 保本证 | Capital Protected Instrument (CPI) | — | — | — | The bank's **Capital Protected Instrument** returns the principal at maturity while linking extra gains to an index. — 银行的**保本证**在到期时退还本金，并将额外收益与指数挂钩。 |
| 4 | 保障安排 | security arrangement | /sɪˈkjʊrəti ərˈeɪnʤmənt/ | — | — | The lender would only release the loan after a **security arrangement** over the company's shares was signed. — 只有在签署了以公司股份作担保的**保障安排**后，贷款人才会放款。 |
| 5 | 被动受托人 | bare trustee | — | — | — | As a **bare trustee**, the nominee holds legal title to the shares but acts solely on the beneficiary's instructions. — 作为**被动受托人**，代名人持有股份的法定所有权，但完全按受益人的指示行事。 |
| 6 | 标的证券 | designated securities | /ˈdɛzɪgˌneɪtɪd sɪˈkjʊrətiz/ | 指定证券 | — | Only **designated securities** approved by the exchange may be sold short under the current rules. — 根据现行规则，只有交易所核准的**标的证券**才可以卖空。 |
| 7 | 并购活动 | mergers and acquisitions | /ˈmərʤərz ənd ˌækwəˈzɪʃənz/ | — | — | Investment banks reported a surge in fees from **mergers and acquisitions** as corporate confidence recovered. — 随着企业信心恢复，投资银行来自**并购活动**的收费大幅增加。 |
| 8 | 不记名证券 | bearer securities | — | — | — | Because **bearer securities** belong to whoever holds the certificate, they must be stored in a secure vault. — 由于**不记名证券**归持有证书的人所有，它们必须存放在安全的保险库中。 |
| 9 | 财政股东 | financial shareholder | — | — | — | The **financial shareholder** was interested mainly in returns and left operational decisions to management. — 这位**财政股东**主要关注投资回报，把经营决策留给管理层。 |
| 10 | 财资企业集团 | financial conglomerate | — | — | — | Regulators monitor each **financial conglomerate** closely because its banking and insurance arms can share risks. — 监管机构密切监察每个**财资企业集团**，因为其银行与保险业务可能互相传导风险。 |
| 11 | 参与者持股纪录报表 | Participant Shareholding Report | — | — | — | The custodian downloaded the **Participant Shareholding Report** to reconcile client positions at the end of the day. — 托管人下载了**参与者持股纪录报表**，以便在日终核对客户持仓。 |
| 12 | 策略投资者 | strategic investor | — | — | 义项语境：市场与交易，对应 strategic investor。 | The company brought in a **strategic investor** whose distribution network could help it expand overseas. — 公司引入了一名**策略投资者**，其分销网络有助于公司拓展海外市场。 |
| 13 | 策略投资者 | tactical investor | — | — | 义项语境：市场与交易，对应 tactical investor。 | A **tactical investor** may buy ahead of an announcement and sell quickly once the price reacts. — **策略投资者**可能在公告前买入，并在股价反应后迅速卖出。 |
| 14 | 产权负担 | encumbrances | — | — | — | The buyer's lawyers confirmed that the shares were free of any **encumbrances** before completion. — 买方律师在交割前确认这些股份不存在任何**产权负担**。 |
| 15 | 场外购回股份 | off-market share repurchase | — | — | — | An **off-market share repurchase** requires shareholder approval because the company buys directly from selected holders. — **场外购回股份**须经股东批准，因为公司是直接向特定持有人购买股份。 |
| 16 | 持股编号 | shareholder Reference Number (SRN) | — | — | — | Please quote your **Shareholder Reference Number** when contacting the registrar about your holding. — 就持股事宜联系过户登记处时，请提供您的**持股编号**。 |
| 17 | 持股人的分布情况 | spread of holders | — | — | — | The exchange reviewed the **spread of holders** to make sure the listing would have an open market. — 交易所审查了**持股人的分布情况**，以确保上市后存在公开市场。 |
| 18 | 筹备董事会 | Preparatory Board | — | — | — | A **Preparatory Board** was formed to oversee the transition until the new board formally took office. — 公司成立了**筹备董事会**，在新董事会正式就任前负责监督过渡工作。 |
| 19 | 除净日 | ex-date | — | — | — | Investors who buy on or after the **ex-date** will not receive the upcoming dividend. — 在**除净日**当天或之后买入的投资者将不会获得即将派发的股息。 |
| 20 | 除净日未经调整之收市价 | ex unadjusted close | — | — | — | The analyst compared the **ex unadjusted close** with the previous day's price to measure the dividend's effect. — 分析师将**除净日未经调整之收市价**与前一日价格比较，以衡量股息的影响。 |
| 21 | 除权 | ex-rights | — | — | — | Once the stock traded **ex-rights**, new buyers were no longer entitled to subscribe for the rights shares. — 股票**除权**交易后，新买家不再有权认购供股股份。 |
| 22 | 除息基准 | ex-dividend basis | — | — | — | Shares bought today trade on an **ex-dividend basis**, so the seller keeps the declared payout. — 今天买入的股份按**除息基准**交易，因此已宣派的股息归卖方所有。 |
| 23 | 传统债券 | plain vanilla bond | — | — | — | Conservative investors still prefer a **plain vanilla bond** with fixed coupons and no embedded options. — 保守型投资者仍偏好票息固定、不附带期权的**传统债券**。 |
| 24 | 存托证券 | depositary receipt | — | 预托证券 | — | The Chinese company listed in New York through a **depositary receipt** programme backed by its local shares. — 这家中国公司通过以其本地股份为基础的**存托证券**计划在纽约上市。 |
| 25 | 打印机分流器 | printer sharer | — | — | — | The branch office installed a **printer sharer** so several trading terminals could use one printer. — 分行安装了一台**打印机分流器**，让几部交易终端可以共用一台打印机。 |
| 26 | 大股东 | substantial shareholder; major shareholder | — | — | — | Any **substantial shareholder** must disclose changes in its holding within the period required by law. — 任何**大股东**均须在法律规定的期限内披露持股变动。 |
| 27 | 大型股 | large cap stock | — | — | — | The portfolio manager favours **large cap stock** for its liquidity and lower volatility. — 基金经理偏爱**大型股**，因为其流动性好、波动较低。 |
| 28 | 代办股份转让系统 | Agency Share Transfer System; Share Transfer Agent System | — | — | 适用/来源：中国大陆。 | Shares of delisted companies may continue to trade on the **Agency Share Transfer System**. — 退市公司的股份可以继续在**代办股份转让系统**上转让。 |
| 29 | 代表委任表格 | proxy form | — | — | — | Shareholders unable to attend the meeting can appoint a representative by returning the **proxy form**. — 无法出席会议的股东可交回**代表委任表格**委任代表。 |
| 30 | 代价 | consideration | — | — | — | The **consideration** for the acquisition will be paid partly in cash and partly in new shares. — 此次收购的**代价**将部分以现金、部分以新股支付。 |
| 31 | 代价股份 | consideration share | — | — | — | The vendor will receive each **consideration share** at the issue price set out in the agreement. — 卖方将按协议所列发行价获得每一股**代价股份**。 |
| 32 | 代收股息 | dividend and debt securities interest collection service fee | — | 债券利息服务费 | — | The clearing house charges a **dividend and debt securities interest collection service fee** for collecting payouts on behalf of participants. — 结算所代参与者收取派息时会收取**代收股息**服务费。 |
| 33 | 代息股份 | scrip entitlement | — | — | — | Shareholders who chose the scrip option will see their **scrip entitlement** credited to their stock accounts. — 选择以股代息的股东，其**代息股份**将存入其股票账户。 |
| 34 | 待转名股份 | “street name” share certificate | — | — | — | The broker kept the **street name share certificate** registered in its nominee's name for easy transfer. — 经纪行把**待转名股份**登记在其代理人名下，以便于转让。 |
| 35 | 贷股人 | stock lender | — | — | — | The **stock lender** earns a fee while its shares are on loan to short sellers. — 股份借给卖空者期间，**贷股人**可以赚取费用。 |
| 36 | 担保权益 | security interest | — | — | — | The bank registered a **security interest** over the pledged shares to protect its loan. — 银行对质押股份登记了**担保权益**，以保障其贷款。 |
| 37 | 登记股东 | registered shareholder | — | — | — | The borrower entered into a **security arrangement** pledging its bond portfolio as collateral. — 借款人订立了**抵押安排**，以其债券组合作为抵押品。 |
| 38 | 抵押安排 | security arrangement | /sɪˈkjʊrəti ərˈeɪnʤmənt/ | — | — | 同「保障安排」（No. 4） |

| 40 | 抵押证券 | asset-backed securities (ABS) | /ˈæset bækt səˈkjʊrətiz/ | — | — | The bank packaged its car loans into **asset-backed securities** and sold them to institutional buyers. — 银行把汽车贷款打包成**抵押证券**，出售给机构买家。 |
| 41 | 递延股 | deferred share | — | — | — | Holders of a **deferred share** receive dividends only after ordinary shareholders have been paid. — **递延股**持有人只有在普通股东获派股息后才能收取股息。 |
| 42 | 电子传递证券信息服务 | Electronic Mailing Stock Information Service (EMSIS) | — | — | — | Investors can subscribe to the **Electronic Mailing Stock Information Service** to receive market updates by email. — 投资者可以订阅**电子传递证券信息服务**，通过电子邮件接收市场资讯。 |
| 43 | 电子证券申报及批核系统 | e-Securities Declaration and Approval System | — | — | — | Brokers submit forms through the **e-Securities Declaration and Approval System** instead of filing paper documents. — 经纪商通过**电子证券申报及批核系统**提交表格，不再递交纸质文件。 |
| 44 | 定期债券 | term bond | — | — | — | Unlike serial bonds, a **term bond** repays all its principal on a single maturity date. — 与分期债券不同，**定期债券**在单一到期日偿还全部本金。 |

| 46 | 董事酬金 | directors’ remuneration | — | — | — | The annual report discloses **directors' remuneration**, including salaries, bonuses and share awards. — 年报披露了**董事酬金**，包括薪金、花红和股份奖励。 |

| 50 | 董事袍金 | directors’ fee | — | — | — | Non-executive directors receive a fixed **directors' fee** for attending board meetings. — 非执行董事出席董事会会议可获得固定的**董事袍金**。 |
| 51 | 冻结股份机制 | securities-on-hold mechanism | — | — | — | Under the **securities-on-hold mechanism**, shares are frozen in the account until the trade is confirmed. — 在**冻结股份机制**下，股份会在账户中冻结，直至交易获确认。 |

| 54 | 短期证券 | short-dated securities | — | — | — | Money market funds invest mainly in **short-dated securities** such as treasury bills. — 货币市场基金主要投资于国库券等**短期证券**。 |

## 监管合规与法律 { #regulation-compliance-law }

| No. | 中国大陆术语 | 英文 | IPA（美） | 其他说法/缩写 | 备注 | 示例句 |
|---:|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 24 个价位规则 | 24-spread rule | — | — | — | Under the **24-spread rule**, a bid cannot be entered more than 24 price steps below the best bid. — 根据**24 个价位规则**，买盘报价不得低于最佳买价超过 24 个价位。 |
| 2 | T + 3 规则 | T + 3 Rule | — | — | 适用/来源：US。 | US brokers once settled trades under the **T + 3 Rule**, three business days after execution. — 美国经纪商过去按**T + 3 规则**结算，即成交后三个营业日交收。 |
| 3 | 安全港 | safe harbour | — | — | — | The forward-looking statements are protected by a **safe harbour** provided they carry adequate cautionary language. — 只要附有充分的警示性措辞，这些前瞻性陈述便受**安全港**条款保护。 |
| 4 | 《保荐人标准守则》 | Model Code for Sponsors | — | — | — | Our sponsor team reviewed the **Model Code for Sponsors** before starting due diligence on the IPO. — 保荐团队在开始 IPO 尽职调查前先研读了《**保荐人标准守则**》。 |
| 5 | 《保障投资者条例》 | Protection of Investors Ordinance | — | — | — | Before the SFO took effect, the **Protection of Investors Ordinance** governed misleading investment advertisements. — 在《证券及期货条例》生效前，误导性投资广告由《**保障投资者条例**》规管。 |
| 6 | 报价规则 | quotation rules | — | — | — | Traders must follow the exchange's **quotation rules** when entering bids and asks into the system. — 交易员在系统中输入买卖盘时必须遵守交易所的**报价规则**。 |
| 7 | 避税 | tax avoidance | — | — | — | Legitimate **tax avoidance** uses lawful structures, but regulators scrutinise schemes lacking commercial substance. — 合法的**避税**利用合规架构，但监管机构会审查缺乏商业实质的安排。 |
| 8 | 标准授权书 | Model Authorisation Letter | — | — | — | The client signed the **Model Authorisation Letter** so the bank could share account data with the broker. — 客户签署了**标准授权书**，让银行可以向经纪商提供账户资料。 |
| 9 | 不当行为 | misconduct | — | — | — | The regulator banned the dealer for **misconduct** after he repeatedly front-ran client orders. — 该交易员多次抢先客户下单，监管机构以**不当行为**为由对其实施禁令。 |
| 10 | 不公平竞争 | uneven playing field | — | — | 参见:level playing field。 | Smaller brokers complained that the new capital rules created an **uneven playing field** favouring large banks. — 小型券商抱怨新资本规则造成**不公平竞争**，对大银行有利。 |
| 11 | 「不公平损害」补救 | “unfair prejudice” remedy | — | — | — | Minority shareholders sought an **“unfair prejudice” remedy** after the board diverted business to a related company. — 董事会将业务转给关联公司后，小股东申请**「不公平损害」补救**。 |
| 12 | 不可抗力 | force majeure | /ˌfɔːrs mæˈʒɝː/ | — | — | The contract's **force majeure** clause excused delays caused by typhoons and trading system outages beyond our control. — 合约中的**不可抗力**条款豁免了因台风及不可控的交易系统故障造成的延误。 |
| 13 | 财务监管 | financial regulation | — | — | — | Stronger **financial regulation** after the crisis forced banks to hold more capital and liquidity. — 危机后更严格的**财务监管**迫使银行持有更多资本和流动资金。 |
| 14 | 财政资源规则 | financial resources rules | — | — | — | Licensed corporations must report their liquid capital monthly under the **financial resources rules**. — 持牌法团须根据**财政资源规则**每月申报其速动资金。 |
| 15 | 《操守准则》 | Code of Conduct | — | — | — | All licensed representatives are expected to act honestly and fairly as required by the **Code of Conduct**. — 所有持牌代表都应按《**操守准则**》的要求诚实公平地行事。 |
| 16 | 操纵价格 | price manipulation | — | — | — | Prosecutors charged the syndicate with **price manipulation** for trading among themselves to inflate the share price. — 检控方指该集团互相交易以推高股价，控以**操纵价格**罪。 |
| 17 | 场内规则 | on-market rules | — | — | — | Under the **on-market rules**, buybacks executed through the exchange must follow price and timing restrictions. — 根据**场内规则**，通过交易所进行的回购须遵守价格及时间限制。 |
| 18 | 场外规则 | off-market rules | — | — | — | An off-exchange share buyback offered to all holders falls under the **off-market rules** and needs shareholder approval. — 向全体股东提出的场外回购属于**场外规则**范围，须经股东批准。 |
| 19 | 诚信责任 | fiduciary duties | — | — | — | Directors owe **fiduciary duties** to the company and must avoid conflicts between personal and corporate interests. — 董事对公司负有**诚信责任**，必须避免个人与公司利益冲突。 |
| 20 | 持续责任 | continuing obligation; on-going obligation | — | — | — | Disclosure of price-sensitive information is a **continuing obligation** for every listed issuer, not a one-off task. — 披露股价敏感资料是每家上市发行人的**持续责任**，而非一次性工作。 |
| 21 | 从价印花税 | ad valorem stamp duty | — | — | — | Share transfers attract **ad valorem stamp duty** calculated as a percentage of the consideration or market value. — 股份转让须缴纳**从价印花税**，按代价或市值的一定比例计算。 |
| 22 | 错误陈述 | misstatement | — | — | — | The auditor found a material **misstatement** in the revenue figures and asked management to restate the accounts. — 审计师发现收入数据存在重大**错误陈述**，要求管理层重列账目。 |
| 23 | 单一牌照制度 | Single Licence Regime | — | — | — | Under the **Single Licence Regime**, one licence can cover several regulated activities for the same firm. — 在**单一牌照制度**下，同一家公司的多项受规管活动可由一个牌照涵盖。 |
| 24 | 递延税项 | deferred tax | — | — | — | Timing differences between accounting profit and taxable profit gave rise to a **deferred tax** liability. — 会计利润与应税利润之间的时间差异产生了一项**递延税项**负债。 |
| 25 | 吊销资格令 | disqualification order | — | — | — | The court issued a **disqualification order** preventing the former director from managing any company for several years. — 法院颁布**吊销资格令**，禁止该前董事在若干年内管理任何公司。 |

| 29 | 法定公益金 | statutory public welfare fund | — | — | 适用/来源：中国大陆。 | Older mainland company law required firms to allocate profit to a **statutory public welfare fund** for staff facilities. — 旧的内地公司法要求企业从利润中提取**法定公益金**用于职工福利设施。 |

## 会计与信息披露 { #accounting-disclosure }

| No. | 中国大陆术语 | 英文 | IPA（美） | 其他说法/缩写 | 备注 | 示例句 |
|---:|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 保留意见 | qualified opinion | — | — | — | The auditors issued a **qualified opinion** because they could not verify the overseas inventory. — 由于无法核实海外存货，审计师出具了**保留意见**。 |
| 2 | 备抵 | allowance | — | — | 适用/来源：会计。 语境：会计。 | We increased the **allowance** for credit losses after several clients fell behind on payments. — 多名客户拖欠付款后，我们提高了信用损失**备抵**。 |
| 3 | 备考报表 | pro forma statement | — | — | — | The circular includes a **pro forma statement** showing the group as if the merger had already closed. — 通函载有一份**备考报表**，假设合并已完成来展示集团状况。 |
| 4 | 备考全面摊薄市盈率 | pro forma fully diluted price / earnings ratio | — | — | — | Analysts compared the **pro forma fully diluted price / earnings ratio** with those of listed peers. — 分析师将**备考全面摊薄市盈率**与上市同业作比较。 |
| 5 | 比例综合法 | proportionate consolidation method | /prəˈpɔrʃənət kənˌsɑləˈdeɪʃən ˈmɛθəd/ | — | — | Under the **proportionate consolidation method**, the company records its share of the joint venture's assets line by line. — 在**比例综合法**下，公司逐项记录其应占合营企业的资产。 |
| 6 | 贬值 | depreciation | — | — | — | The sharp **depreciation** of the local currency cut the value of the fund's overseas holdings in dollar terms. — 本币大幅**贬值**，令基金海外持仓以美元计的价值缩水。 |
| 7 | 变现 | realisation | — | — | — | Gains are recognised only upon **realisation**, when the shares are actually sold. — 收益只在**变现**时确认，即股份实际出售之时。 |
| 8 | 标准会计准则 | standard accounting practice | — | — | — | The accounts were prepared in line with **standard accounting practice** applicable in Hong Kong. — 账目按香港适用的**标准会计准则**编制。 |
| 9 | 拨备 | provision | — | — | — | The bank made a large **provision** against its property loan portfolio this quarter. — 银行本季度为其物业贷款组合作出大额**拨备**。 |
| 10 | 拨款 | appropriation | — | — | — | The board approved an **appropriation** of retained earnings to fund the new dividend reserve. — 董事会批准从留存盈利中**拨款**设立新的股息储备。 |
| 11 | 不动账户费 | dormant account fee | — | 不动户口费 | — | The brokerage charges a **dormant account fee** if there is no trading activity for an extended period. — 若账户长期无交易，券商会收取**不动账户费**。 |
| 12 | 财务报表审阅计划 | Financial Statements Review Programme | — | 财务报表审阅计画 | — | Under the **Financial Statements Review Programme**, regulators examine listed issuers' published accounts for compliance issues. — 在**财务报表审阅计划**下，监管机构审查上市发行人已发布的账目是否存在合规问题。 |
| 13 | 财务汇报 | financial reporting | — | 财务申报 | — | Strong **financial reporting** controls help investors trust the numbers a company publishes. — 健全的**财务汇报**监控有助投资者信任公司公布的数字。 |
| 14 | 财务汇报检讨委员会 | Financial Reporting Review Committee (FRRC); Financial Reporting Review Panel | — | — | — | The complaint was referred to the **Financial Reporting Review Committee** for an independent examination of the accounts. — 该投诉已转交**财务汇报检讨委员会**，对账目进行独立审查。 |
| 15 | 财务汇报局 | The Financial Reporting Council (FRC) | — | — | — | **The Financial Reporting Council** oversees the regulation of auditors of public interest entities in Hong Kong. — **财务汇报局**负责监管香港公众利益实体核数师。 |
| 16 | 财务业绩 | financial results | — | — | — | The company will release its interim **financial results** after the market closes on Thursday. — 公司将于周四收市后公布中期**财务业绩**。 |
| 17 | 财务摘要报告 | summary financial report | — | — | — | Shareholders may elect to receive a **summary financial report** instead of the full annual report. — 股东可选择收取**财务摘要报告**，而非完整年报。 |
| 18 | 财政年度 | financial year | — | — | — | Our **financial year** ends on 31 March, so the annual audit starts in April. — 我们的**财政年度**于3月31日结束，所以年度审计在4月开始。 |
| 19 | 残值 | salvage value | — | — | — | The machine is depreciated down to its estimated **salvage value** over ten years. — 该机器在十年内折旧至其估计**残值**。 |
| 20 | 「拆骨专家」 | asset stripper | — | — | — | The fund was labelled an **asset stripper** after it bought the firm and sold off its best divisions. — 该基金收购公司后变卖其最好的业务部门，被冠以「**拆骨专家**」之名。 |
| 21 | 拆卖资产 | asset stripping | — | — | — | Employees feared the takeover would lead to **asset stripping** rather than long-term investment. — 员工担心这次收购会导致**拆卖资产**，而非长期投资。 |
| 22 | 产品资料概要 | Product Key Fact Statement | — | — | — | Before buying the fund, read the **Product Key Fact Statement** to understand its key risks and fees. — 购买基金前，请阅读**产品资料概要**，了解其主要风险和费用。 |
| 23 | 成本效益 | cost effectiveness | — | — | — | Management reviewed the **cost effectiveness** of outsourcing the back-office settlement work. — 管理层检讨了外判后台交收工作的**成本效益**。 |
| 24 | 承前 | brought forward | — | — | 适用/来源：会计。 语境：会计。 | The balance **brought forward** from last month should match the closing balance on the previous statement. — 上月**承前**结余应与上期结单的期末结余一致。 |
| 25 | 持权成本 | cost of carry | — | — | 适用/来源：期权。 | For index options, the **cost of carry** reflects interest costs minus expected dividends. — 就指数期权而言，**持权成本**反映利息成本减去预期股息。 |
| 26 | 持续经营 | going concern | — | — | 适用/来源：会计。 语境：会计。 | The auditor raised doubts about the company's ability to continue as a **going concern**. — 审计师对公司能否**持续经营**表示怀疑。 |
| 27 | 持续披露及资料传递政策 | Continuous Disclosure and Communication Policy | /kənˈtɪnjuəs dɪˈskloʊʒər ənd kəmˌjunəˈkeɪʃən ˈpɑləsi/ | — | — | The board adopted a **Continuous Disclosure and Communication Policy** to ensure timely release of inside information. — 董事会采纳了**持续披露及资料传递政策**，以确保内幕消息及时发布。 |
| 28 | 持有成本 | cost of carry | — | — | — | 同「持权成本」（No. 25） |
| 29 | 储备 | reserve | — | — | — | The insurer transferred part of its profit to a **reserve** to cover future claims. — 保险公司将部分利润转入**储备**，以应付未来索偿。 |
| 30 | 存货 | inventory | — | — | — | Rising **inventory** levels suggest that demand for the retailer's products is slowing. — **存货**水平上升，显示市场对该零售商产品的需求正在放缓。 |
| 31 | 存款准备金率 | reserve ratio / reserve requirement / reserve requirement ratio | — | — | — | The central bank cut the **reserve requirement ratio** to release more liquidity into the banking system. — 央行下调**存款准备金率**，向银行体系释放更多流动性。 |
| 32 | 代理人账户 | nominee account | — | 代理人户口 | — | Her shares are held in a **nominee account**, so the broker appears as the registered holder. — 她的股份存放于**代理人账户**，因此登记持有人显示为经纪行。 |

| 35 | 递耗资产 | decaying asset | — | — | — | A mine is a classic **decaying asset**, since its reserves shrink as they are extracted. — 矿山是典型的**递耗资产**，因为储量随开采不断减少。 |
| 36 | 调整表 | statement of adjustments | — | 调整声明 | — | The prospectus includes a **statement of adjustments** reconciling the reported figures to the accountants' report. — 招股章程载有一份**调整表**，将已公布数字与会计师报告进行对账。 |
| 37 | 调整后有形资产净值 | adjusted net tangible assets | — | — | — | The company's **adjusted net tangible assets** per share are disclosed in the listing document. — 公司每股**调整后有形资产净值**已在上市文件中披露。 |
| 38 | 调整后资产净值 | adjusted net assets | — | — | — | After revaluing its properties, the group reported higher **adjusted net assets** in the circular. — 集团重估物业后，在通函中呈报了更高的**调整后资产净值**。 |
| 39 | 独立信托账户 | segregated trust account | — | 独立信托户口 | — | Client money must be kept in a **segregated trust account**, separate from the firm's own funds. — 客户款项必须存放于**独立信托账户**，与公司自有资金分开。 |
| 40 | 对账 | reconciliation | /ˌrekənsɪliˈeɪʃən/ | — | — | The operations team performs a daily **reconciliation** between internal records and the custodian's statements. — 运营团队每日对内部记录和托管人结单进行**对账**。 |
| 41 | 法定储备 | statutory reserve | — | — | — | Local regulations require the subsidiary to set aside part of its profit as a **statutory reserve**. — 当地法规要求子公司将部分利润拨作**法定储备**。 |
| 42 | 非经常项目 | extraordinary item | /ˌɛkstrəˈɔrdəˌnɛri ˈaɪtəm/ | — | — | The gain from the factory fire insurance was reported as an **extraordinary item**. — 工厂火灾保险赔偿所得被列为**非经常项目**。 |
| 43 | 非认可负债 | non-ranking liabilities | — | — | — | Subordinated loans approved by the regulator may be treated as **non-ranking liabilities** in the capital computation. — 经监管机构批准的后偿贷款，在资本计算中可视为**非认可负债**。 |

## 保证金与风险管理 { #margin-risk-management }

| No. | 中国大陆术语 | 英文 | IPA（美） | 其他说法/缩写 | 备注 | 示例句 |
|---:|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | alpha 系数 | alpha coefficient | — | — | — | The fund's **alpha coefficient** shows how much it beat its benchmark after adjusting for risk. — 该基金的 **alpha 系数**显示了其经风险调整后跑赢基准的幅度。 |
| 2 | Delta 式对冲 | delta hedge | — | — | — | The market maker ran a **delta hedge** by selling shares as the call options moved deeper into the money. — 随着认购期权越来越价内，做市商卖出股票进行 **Delta 式对冲**。 |
| 3 | 保证金 | margin | — | 孖展；按金 | — | Investors must post **margin** with their broker before opening a leveraged futures position. — 投资者在开立杠杆期货仓位前，必须向经纪商缴付**保证金**。 |
| 4 | 保证金保险范围比率 | margin coverage ratio | — | — | — | Risk officers review each client's **margin coverage ratio** daily to see whether collateral still covers exposure. — 风险人员每天检查每位客户的**保证金保险范围比率**，看抵押品是否仍足以覆盖风险敞口。 |
| 5 | 保证金比例 | margin ratio | — | 按金比例 | — | When the **margin ratio** falls below the maintenance level, the broker will ask the client to add funds. — 当**保证金比例**跌破维持水平时，经纪商会要求客户追加资金。 |
| 6 | 保证金对销 | margin offset | — | 按金对销 | — | Because the long and short positions are correlated, the clearing house grants a **margin offset** between them. — 由于多空仓位相关，结算所对两者给予**保证金对销**。 |
| 7 | 保证金覆盖率 | margin coverage ratio | — | 孖展覆盖率 | — | 同「保证金保险范围比率」（No. 4） |
| 8 | 保证金豁免额 | margin credit | — | 按金豁免额 | — | The client's hedged positions generated a **margin credit** that reduced the total collateral required. — 客户的对冲仓位产生了**保证金豁免额**，降低了所需抵押品总额。 |
| 9 | 保证金价值 | margin value | — | — | — | The broker assigns each eligible stock a **margin value** based on its price and the applicable haircut. — 经纪商根据价格和适用扣减率为每只合资格股票设定**保证金价值**。 |
| 10 | 保证金间距 | margin interval | — | 按金间距 | — | The exchange widened the **margin interval** after volatility in the index futures market rose sharply. — 指数期货市场波动急升后，交易所扩大了**保证金间距**。 |
| 11 | 保证金减免 | margin relief | — | 按金减免 | — | Members holding offsetting spreads may apply for **margin relief** under the clearing house's rules. — 持有对销跨期仓位的会员可根据结算所规则申请**保证金减免**。 |
| 12 | 保证金交易 | margin trading | — | 按金交易 | — | **Margin trading** magnifies both gains and losses, so beginners should use it with great caution. — **保证金交易**会同时放大收益和亏损，新手应非常谨慎使用。 |
| 13 | 保证金借贷活动 | margin financing | — | — | — | Regulators are watching **margin financing** closely as leveraged buying in small-cap stocks keeps growing. — 随着小盘股杠杆买盘持续增加，监管机构正密切关注**保证金借贷活动**。 |
| 14 | 保证金客户 | margin client | — | 孖展客户 | — | Every **margin client** must sign a separate agreement acknowledging the risks of borrowing to invest. — 每位**保证金客户**都必须另签协议，确认了解借钱投资的风险。 |
| 15 | 保证金买卖 | margin trading | — | 孖展买卖 | — | 同「保证金交易」（No. 12） |
| 16 | 保证金融资 | margin financing | — | 孖展融资 | — | 同「保证金借贷活动」（No. 13） |
| 17 | 保证金证券交易账户 | Margin Securities Trading Account | /ˈmɑrʤən sɪˈkjʊrətiz ˈtreɪdɪŋ əˈkaʊnt/ | 保证金证券交易户口 | — | She opened a **Margin Securities Trading Account** so she could buy shares partly with borrowed money. — 她开立了**保证金证券交易账户**，以便部分用借来的资金买股票。 |
| 18 | 保证人 | guarantor | — | — | — | The bank required a **guarantor** to sign the facility letter before releasing the loan to the startup. — 银行要求一名**保证人**签署融资函件，才向这家初创公司发放贷款。 |
| 19 | 变价调整 | variation adjustment | — | — | — | A **variation adjustment** was made to the account to reflect the day's change in contract prices. — 账户作出了**变价调整**，以反映当日合约价格的变动。 |
| 20 | 标准组合风险分析 | Standard Portfolio Analysis of Risk (SPAN) | — | — | — | Many futures exchanges use **SPAN** to calculate margin by simulating losses across a whole portfolio. — 许多期货交易所采用**标准组合风险分析**，通过模拟整个组合的亏损来计算保证金。 |
| 21 | 补仓通知 | margin call | — | — | — | After the stock plunged, he received a **margin call** asking him to deposit more cash by the next morning. — 股价暴跌后，他收到**补仓通知**，要求次日早上前存入更多现金。 |
| 22 | 不定额供款 | variable contribution | — | — | — | Employees can make a **variable contribution** to their pension fund on top of the mandatory amount. — 雇员可以在强制供款之外，向退休基金作出**不定额供款**。 |
| 23 | 财务风险 | financial risk; financial exposure | — | — | — | Heavy borrowing in foreign currency increased the company's **financial risk** when rates started to rise. — 利率开始上升时，大量外币借款加大了该公司的**财务风险**。 |
| 24 | 偿债能力风险 | insolvency risk | — | — | — | Analysts flagged **insolvency risk** after the developer missed several bond coupon payments. — 该开发商多次未能支付债券利息后，分析师提示了其**偿债能力风险**。 |
| 25 | 持仓风险调整 | position risk adjustment | — | — | — | The firm booked a **position risk adjustment** to reflect potential losses on its proprietary trading book. — 公司计提了**持仓风险调整**，以反映自营交易账簿的潜在亏损。 |
| 26 | 大额持仓汇报协议 | Large Exposure Report Agreement | — | — | — | Participants with sizeable positions must sign the **Large Exposure Report Agreement** to report their holdings. — 持有大额仓位的参与者须签署**大额持仓汇报协议**，以汇报其持仓。 |
| 27 | 贷款抵押证券 | collateralised loan obligation (CLO) | — | — | — | A **CLO** pools leveraged corporate loans and sells tranches with different levels of risk to investors. — **贷款抵押证券**将杠杆企业贷款打包，并向投资者出售不同风险等级的分层。 |

| 120 | 主权风险 | sovereign risk | — | — | — | Investors demand higher yields on the country's bonds because of rising **sovereign risk**. — 由于**主权风险**上升，投资者要求该国债券提供更高收益率。 |
| 121 | 追补保证金 | margin call | — | 追补按金 | — | 同「补仓通知」（No. 21） |
| 122 | 资不抵债 | insolvency | /ɪnˈsɑːlvənsi/ | — | — | 同「无偿债能力」（No. 96） |
| 123 | 资产负债表以外之承担 | off-balance sheet exposure | — | 项目 | — | Analysts examined the bank's **off-balance sheet exposure** to guarantees and credit lines. — 分析师审视了该银行在担保和信贷额度方面的**资产负债表以外之承担**。 |
| 124 | 综合风险 | composite risk | — | — | — | The report assessed the fund's **composite risk** by combining market, credit and liquidity factors. — 报告结合市场、信贷和流动性因素评估该基金的**综合风险**。 |
| 125 | 总保证金价值 | total margin value | — | 总按金价值 | — | The **total margin value** of all collateral must exceed the client's outstanding loan balance. — 所有抵押品的**总保证金价值**必须高于客户的未偿贷款余额。 |
| 126 | 总额保证金计算 | gross margining | — | 总额按金计算 | — | Under **gross margining**, each client's positions are margined separately without netting. — 采用**总额保证金计算**时，每位客户的仓位分别计算保证金，不作净额对销。 |

| 10 | 承办银行 | administering bank | — | — | — | The **administering bank** collects interest from the borrower and distributes it to all lenders in the facility. — **承办银行**向借款人收取利息，并分配给该贷款中的所有贷款人。 |
| 11 | 承兑 | acceptance | — | — | — | Once the importer's bank signed its **acceptance** on the draft, the exporter could discount the bill. — 进口商的银行在汇票上签署**承兑**后，出口商便可将汇票贴现。 |

| 13 | 贷款股 | loan stock | — | 贷款债券 | — | The company raised funds by issuing **loan stock** that pays a fixed interest rate until maturity. — 公司通过发行**贷款股**筹资，到期前支付固定利息。 |
| 14 | 贷款融通 | loan facility | — | — | — | The bank extended a revolving **loan facility** so the firm could draw cash whenever it needed working capital. — 银行提供了循环**贷款融通**，让公司可在需要营运资金时随时提款。 |
