| 18 | 调息按揭 | adjustable rate mortgage (ARM) | /əˈʤəstəbəl reɪt ˈmɔrgɪʤ/ | — | — | With an **adjustable rate mortgage**, her monthly payment rose when the benchmark rate went up. — 采用**调息按揭**后，基准利率一上调，她的月供就增加了。 |

| 20 | 短期举债 | short term borrowings | — | 借贷 | — | The CFO reduced **short term borrowings** by refinancing them with a five-year bond. — 首席财务官用五年期债券再融资，减少了**短期举债**。 |
| 21 | 短期资金 | short-term money | — | — | — | Corporate treasurers park **short-term money** in deposits that can be withdrawn within days. — 企业司库把**短期资金**存放在几天内即可提取的存款中。 |
| 22 | 短期资金借贷市场 | money market | — | — | — | Large companies issue commercial paper in the **money market** to cover seasonal funding needs. — 大公司在**短期资金借贷市场**发行商业票据，以满足季节性资金需求。 |
| 23 | 反通货膨胀 | disinflation | — | — | — | Prices are still rising, but **disinflation** means they are rising more slowly than last year. — 物价仍在上涨，但**反通货膨胀**意味着涨幅比去年放缓。 |
| 24 | 放宽利率管制 | deregulation of interest rates | — | — | — | The **deregulation of interest rates** allowed banks to compete for deposits by offering higher returns. — **放宽利率管制**后，银行可以通过提供更高回报来争取存款。 |

| 26 | 浮息存款证 | floating rate certificate of deposit (FRCD) | — | — | — | The bank issued a **floating rate certificate of deposit** whose coupon resets every three months against HIBOR. — 银行发行了一种**浮息存款证**，其票息每三个月参照 HIBOR 重新厘定。 |

| 28 | 负债比率 | debt-to-equity ratio | — | — | — | Lenders grew cautious because the company's **debt-to-equity ratio** had climbed well above the industry average. — 由于公司的**负债比率**已远高于行业平均水平，贷款人变得谨慎。 |
| 29 | 负债日 | indebtedness date | — | — | — | The prospectus discloses all borrowings outstanding as at the **indebtedness date**. — 招股章程披露了截至**负债日**所有未偿还的借款。 |

| 31 | 杠杆式外汇交易 | leveraged foreign exchange trading | — | 买卖 | — | **Leveraged foreign exchange trading** can magnify both profits and losses from small currency moves. — **杠杆式外汇交易**会把汇率小幅波动带来的盈利和亏损同时放大。 |
| 32 | 杠杆式外汇买卖介绍代理人 | leveraged foreign exchange introducing agent | — | — | — | A **leveraged foreign exchange introducing agent** refers clients to a licensed dealer but cannot hold client money. — **杠杆式外汇买卖介绍代理人**把客户介绍给持牌交易商，但不得持有客户款项。 |

| 36 | 更紧密经贸关系安排 | Closer Economic Partnership Arrangement (CEPA) | — | — | — | Under **CEPA**, many Hong Kong service providers gained preferential access to the mainland market. — 根据**更紧密经贸关系安排**，许多香港服务提供者获得了进入内地市场的优惠待遇。 |

| 40 | 国际外汇同业联会 | Association Cambiste Internationale | — | — | — | Many dealers belong to the **Association Cambiste Internationale**, which promotes professional standards in foreign exchange. — 许多交易员是**国际外汇同业联会**的会员，该会致力于推广外汇业的专业标准。 |

| 43 | 国民生产总值 | gross national product (GNP) | — | — | — | **Gross national product** includes income that residents earn abroad, unlike GDP. — 与 GDP 不同，**国民生产总值**包括居民在海外赚取的收入。 |

| 56 | 货币资产 | monetary assets | — | — | — | Cash and receivables are classified as **monetary assets** because their value is fixed in currency terms. — 现金和应收款被归类为**货币资产**，因为其价值以固定货币金额计。 |
| 57 | 基线与排放权交易 | baseline and credit | — | — | — | Under a **baseline and credit** scheme, firms that cut emissions below their baseline earn credits they can sell. — 在**基线与排放权交易**机制下，减排至基线以下的企业可获得可出售的排放额度。 |
| 58 | 即日回购协议 | intraday repurchase agreement | — | — | 适用/来源：银行。 语境：银行。 | Settlement banks use an **intraday repurchase agreement** to obtain funds in the morning and unwind it by day end. — 结算银行利用**即日回购协议**在早上取得资金，并于日终平仓。 |
| 59 | 即日流动资金 | intraday liquidity | — | — | — | Without enough **intraday liquidity**, a bank may have to delay large payments until incoming funds arrive. — 若**即日流动资金**不足，银行可能需要推迟大额付款，直至收到款项。 |
| 60 | 记账式国库券 | Registered Treasury | — | — | 适用/来源：中国大陆。 | A **Registered Treasury** is recorded electronically in the investor's account rather than issued on paper. — **记账式国库券**以电子方式记入投资者账户，而非发行纸质凭证。 |
| 61 | 价内 | in-the-money | — | — | — | The call option is **in-the-money** because the share price is above the strike price. — 该认购期权处于**价内**，因为股价高于行使价。 |
| 62 | 价外 | out-of-the-money | — | — | — | The warrant expired **out-of-the-money**, so holders received nothing. — 该认股证在**价外**状态下到期，持有人一无所得。 |
| 63 | 价值稳定的货币 | hard currency | — | — | — | Investors in unstable economies often save in a **hard currency** such as the US dollar. — 经济不稳定国家的投资者常以美元等**价值稳定的货币**储蓄。 |
| 64 | 价值状况 | moneyness | — | — | — | The trader checked each option's **moneyness** to see how far the strike was from the spot price. — 交易员检查每只期权的**价值状况**，看行使价离现价有多远。 |

| 70 | 紧急贷款者 | lender of last resort | — | — | — | The central bank acts as **lender of last resort** when solvent banks cannot borrow elsewhere. — 当有偿付能力的银行无法从别处借款时，央行便充当**紧急贷款者**。 |

| 72 | 经济增加值 | economic value added | — | — | — | Managers are rewarded based on **economic value added**, which measures profit above the cost of capital. — 管理层按**经济增加值**获得奖励，该指标衡量超过资本成本的利润。 |
| 73 | 救市贷款 | Lifeboat Loan | — | — | — | Regulators arranged a **Lifeboat Loan** to keep the troubled bank operating while a buyer was found. — 监管机构安排了**救市贷款**，在寻找买家期间维持陷入困境的银行运作。 |

| 76 | 利率衔接 | interest collar; interest rate collar | — | — | — | The borrower bought an **interest rate collar** to cap its funding cost while giving up some benefit from falling rates. — 借款人买入**利率衔接**，以锁定融资成本上限，同时放弃部分利率下跌的好处。 |
| 77 | 利息补贴 | interest rate subsidy | — | — | 适用/来源：中国大陆。 | The government provides an **interest rate subsidy** on loans to small farmers. — 政府为小农贷款提供**利息补贴**。 |
| 78 | 联系汇率 | pegged exchange rate | — | — | — | Hong Kong's **pegged exchange rate** keeps the local dollar trading within a narrow band against the US dollar. — 香港的**联系汇率**使港元兑美元维持在一个狭窄区间内买卖。 |
| 79 | 联系汇率制 | pegged rate system | — | — | — | 同「挂钩汇率制」（No. 39） |
| 80 | 流动性 | liquidity | /lɪˈkwɪdəti/ | 流通性 | — | Small-cap stocks often lack **liquidity**, so large orders can move their prices sharply. — 小型股往往缺乏**流动性**，大额订单可能令股价大幅波动。 |

| No. | 中国大陆术语 | 英文 | IPA（美） | 其他说法/缩写 | 备注 | 示例句 |
|---:|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 按价格排序的传送专线 | market-by-price feed | — | — | — | Our quant desk subscribes to the **market-by-price feed** to see aggregated volume at each price level. — 我们的量化交易台订阅了**按价格排序的传送专线**，以查看每个价位的汇总挂单量。 |
| 2 | 按买卖盘排序的传送专线 | market-by-order feed | — | — | — | A **market-by-order feed** shows every individual order in the queue, not just totals per price. — **按买卖盘排序的传送专线**会显示队列中的每一张买卖盘，而不只是各价位的总量。 |

| 98 | 移动平均值背驰指标 | Moving Average Convergence-Divergence (MACD) | — | — | — | The chartist bought when the **MACD** line crossed above its signal line. — 图表分析师在**移动平均值背驰指标**线向上穿越信号线时买入。 |
| 99 | 已终止经营之业务 | discontinued operations | — | — | — | Losses from **discontinued operations** were reported separately from the core business results. — **已终止经营之业务**的亏损与核心业务业绩分开列报。 |
| 100 | 以报价 | quote-driven | — | 开盘带动 | — | Most bond markets are **quote-driven**, with dealers posting prices rather than matching orders centrally. — 大部分债券市场是**以报价**驱动的，由交易商挂出价格，而非集中撮合买卖盘。 |
| 101 | 易变性 | volatility | /ˌvɑːləˈtɪləti/ | — | — | 同「波动性」（No. 11） |
| 102 | 引申波幅 | implied volatility | — | — | — | Rising **implied volatility** made the options more expensive even though the stock price was flat. — 尽管股价持平，**引申波幅**上升令期权变得更贵。 |
| 103 | 营运 | operating lease | — | 营业租赁 | — | 同「经营租赁」（No. 49） |
| 104 | 影响股价资料 | price-sensitive information | — | — | — | 同「股价敏感资料」（No. 34） |
| 105 | 证券交易自动报价系统 | Securities Trading Automated Quotation System (STAQS) | /sɪˈkjʊrətiz ˈtreɪdɪŋ ˈɔtəˌmeɪtɪd kwoʊˈteɪʃən ˈsɪstəm/ | — | 适用/来源：中国大陆。 | The **Securities Trading Automated Quotation System** was an early electronic platform for trading legal-person shares in mainland China. — **证券交易自动报价系统**是中国内地早期用于买卖法人股的电子平台。 |
| 106 | 指示价格 | indicative price | — | — | — | The dealer gave an **indicative price**, noting it might change before a firm quote. — 交易商给出了**指示价格**，并说明在确实报价前可能会变动。 |
| 107 | 指数参与单位 | index participation unit | — | — | — | Holding an **index participation unit** gives investors exposure to the whole index. — 持有**指数参与单位**可让投资者参与整个指数的表现。 |
| 108 | 指数存托证券 | index depositary receipt; index share | — | 指数预托证券 | — | An **index depositary receipt** trades like a stock while tracking a benchmark index. — **指数存托证券**像股票一样买卖，同时追踪基准指数。 |
| 109 | 指数加权移动平均方法 | exponentially-weighted moving average | /ˌekspəˈnenʃəli ˈweɪtɪd ˈmuːvɪŋ ˈævərɪdʒ/ | — | — | The risk model uses an **exponentially-weighted moving average** so recent returns count more. — 风险模型采用**指数加权移动平均方法**，使近期回报的权重更大。 |
| 110 | 指数套戥 | index arbitrage | — | — | — | The desk ran **index arbitrage**, buying futures and selling the underlying stocks when prices diverged. — 交易台进行**指数套戥**，在价格出现偏离时买入期货并沽出相关股票。 |
| 111 | 指数债券 | index bond | — | — | — | The coupon on the **index bond** is linked to inflation, protecting investors' real returns. — 该**指数债券**的票息与通胀挂钩，保障投资者的实际回报。 |
| 112 | 中证流通指数 | China Securities Free Floating Index | /ˈʧaɪnə sɪˈkjʊrətiz fri ˈfloʊtɪŋ ˈɪndɛks/ | — | — | The **China Securities Free Floating Index** weights stocks by shares actually available for trading. — **中证流通指数**按实际可流通股份为股票加权。 |
| 113 | 中证指数有限公司 | China Securities Index Co., Ltd | — | — | — | **China Securities Index Co., Ltd** compiles and maintains the CSI series of indices. — **中证指数有限公司**负责编制及维护中证系列指数。 |
| 114 | 组播数据传送专线 | multicast datafeed | — | — | — | A **multicast datafeed** sends one stream to many subscribers at once, saving bandwidth. — **组播数据传送专线**将同一数据流同时发送给多个订阅者，节省带宽。 |
| 115 | 最低价格变动 | minimum price fluctuation; tick (size) | — | — | — | The stock's **tick size** is one cent, so prices can only move in one-cent steps. — 该股的**最低价格变动**为一分，所以价格只能以一分为单位变动。 |
| 116 | 最低上落价位 | minimum price fluctuation; tick (size) | — | — | — | 同「最低价格变动」（No. 115） |
| 117 | 最佳价格 | best price | — | — | — | Brokers have a duty to seek the **best price** available when executing client orders. — 经纪商在执行客户买卖盘时有责任争取可得的**最佳价格**。 |
| 118 | 最少开价数量 | minimum quote quantity | — | — | — | Market makers must quote at least the **minimum quote quantity** so their prices are meaningful. — 庄家的报价必须不少于**最少开价数量**，其价格才具实际意义。 |

## 附录：香港市场术语 { #hong-kong-appendix }

> 本附录保留香港市场的官方制度、机构、产品、规则、系统及历史名称，不将其等同于中国大陆制度。

| 36 | 《中央结算系统一般规则》 | General Rules of CCASS | — | — | 香港市场专有术语。 | Every clearing participant is bound by the **General Rules of CCASS** once admitted to the system. — 每名结算参与者一经获接纳加入系统，即受**《中央结算系统一般规则》**约束。 |
| 37 | 《中央结算系统运作程序规则》 | CCASS Operational Procedures | — | — | 香港市场专有术语。 | The back office follows the **CCASS Operational Procedures** when settling stock transfers each day. — 后台部门每日交收股票过户时都会遵循**《中央结算系统运作程序规则》**。 |
| 38 | 咨询委员会 | Advisory Committee | — | — | 香港市场专有术语。 适用/来源：香港证监会(SFC)。 语境：SFC。 | The SFC's **Advisory Committee** gives the regulator views on major policy matters affecting the market. — 证监会的**咨询委员会**就影响市场的重大政策事宜向监管机构提供意见。 |

### 交易与结算基础设施

| No. | 中国大陆术语 | 英文 | IPA（美） | 其他说法/缩写 | 备注 | 示例句 |
|---:|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | CCASS 网上股权披露服务 | Online CCASS Shareholding Disclosure Service | — | — | 香港市场专有术语。 | Investors can check a company's custodian holdings through the **Online CCASS Shareholding Disclosure Service**. — 投资者可通过**CCASS 网上股权披露服务**查询某公司股份在各托管机构的持仓。 |

### 产品与行情

| 4 | 香港董事学会 | The Hong Kong Institute of Directors | — | — | 香港市场专有术语。 | The new board member took a corporate governance course offered by **The Hong Kong Institute of Directors**. — 这位新董事修读了**香港董事学会**开设的公司治理课程。 |
